DAVID-TENSOR24-THE-TRIGGERS_CHECKS.txt: the checks of DAVID-TENSOR24-THE-TRIGGERS.md (law 4's top and bottom, and the many bangs).
A helper's probes (t24h.py the run-time harness: the head's own acts with each variant assigned at run time, no file of the
repository edited), rerun by David on toys-5 with its own src by the helper's rerun.sh (the exact commands, below), each
output compared with the helper's committed one by rerun.sh's last step, whose report comes first. Then every rerun output,
the helper's draft with its proofs in full, and the sources. Integers only, beyond the timing prints.

== rerun.sh (the exact commands) ==
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# The triggers draft (DERIVATION.md), rerun: every output kept in out/ and p0_harness_check.txt, written again by the
# same probe with the same arguments into out_rerun/<same name>, one command per output (a command that wrote a state
# hash file h_*.txt or an X series xs_*.txt beside its text writes them again beside it), sequentially, each under
# nice -n 10, cheapest first and the three 900-tick hole runs last; then a compare. It changes into the triggers
# folder, writes only out_rerun/, and edits no probe, no output and nothing in the repository.
#
# 48 commands (58 python runs: three commands group the runs that wrote one file together), producing 70 files:
# the 46 text outputs of out/ but one, its 21 hash files and 3 X series, and p0_harness_check.txt.
#
# Not rerun: out/p3_edge_nofine.txt. It was written by an earlier revision of p3_edge.py whose fourth mutant was
# 'nofine' (the carry's fine tooth reset each tick). The current p3_edge.py has 'spring' in its place
# (out/p3_edge.txt), so no current command writes that file; the two files differ in their last two lines alone.
#
# One argument made explicit: out/p2_bottom_a.txt was written before p2_bottom.py took 'bottoms=', when its default
# list was floor19,floorG,wmin,none; the current default adds smin, so its rerun names the old list.
#
# The compare at the end prints IDENTICAL or DIFFERS per file. It ignores exactly these, in both files, and nothing
# else (each is replaced by a placeholder, or the line dropped, before the comparison):
#   1. the shell's time report that the outputs written under bash's `time` carry: the lines 'real', 'user' and 'sys'
#      with a duration in minutes and seconds (regex ^(real|user|sys)\t[0-9]+m[0-9]+\.[0-9]+s$), and the one empty
#      line the shell prints right before 'real';
#   2. the run times the probes print: course.py's ' in NN s' in its line '  ran N of M ticks in NN s', and the
#      ' (NN s)' at the end of a line of p0_harness_check.py, p1_rate0.py and p2_bottom.py.
# For a file that DIFFERS it adds, as information only, whether the two are the same once the defaults and lines
# that course.py and t24h.py print since some outputs were written are dropped:
#   - in the switch lists course.py and p4_tst.py print: the later keys at their defaults,
#     ', "uround": "plus1"', ', "tflows": "even"', ", 'uround': 'plus1'" and ", 'tflows': 'even'";
#   - course.py's later summary lines '  least X at a tick's end ...' and '  pairs of whole swings with the same X
#     course ...', dropped from the rerun only where the kept output has no such line;
# and the first lines of the difference.
#
# Expected: IDENTICAL for 47 of the 70 files: the 21 h_* and 3 xs_* files, p0, the 4 p1, the 5 p2, p3_edge,
# p4b, p4c, p4_uround_live, p5, and the 8 course.py outputs written since the 'tflows' switch (V5M_a_tflows,
# V500k_a_tflows, headbang, headbang_tflows, world_hole_tflows_even/odd, world_nature_tflows_even/odd).
# DIFFERS for 23, each "the same once the later defaults and lines are dropped": the 20 course.py outputs
# written before those keys and lines existed, and p4_tst.txt, p4_tst_mutant.txt and p4_tst_flows_odd.txt.
# About 45 minutes in all (up to an hour on a busy machine); the three hole runs about 4 minutes each.

set -u
cd /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/triggers || exit 1

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the compare
nice -n 10 /home/user/Universe24/venv314/bin/python -I - <<'EOF'
import difflib
import os
import re

NOT_RERUN = {
    "out/p3_edge_nofine.txt": "written by an earlier p3_edge.py, whose fourth mutant 'nofine' is 'spring' now",
}
TIME_LINE = re.compile(r"^(real|user|sys)\t[0-9]+m[0-9]+\.[0-9]+s$")
LATER_KEYS = [', "uround": "plus1"', ', "tflows": "even"', ", 'uround': 'plus1'", ", 'tflows': 'even'"]
LATER_LINES = ("  least X at a tick's end ", "  pairs of whole swings with the same X course")


def read(path):
    with open(path) as f:
        return f.read().split("\n")


def without_times(lines):
    out = []
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if TIME_LINE.match(line):
            continue
        if line == "" and i + 1 < len(lines) and TIME_LINE.match(lines[i + 1]) and lines[i + 1].startswith("real"):
            continue
        line = re.sub(r"^(  ran [0-9]+ of [0-9]+ ticks) in [0-9]+ s", r"\1 in <time> s", line)
        line = re.sub(r" \([0-9]+ s\)$", " (<time> s)", line)
        out.append(line)
    return out


def without_later(kept, rerun):
    def keys(lines):
        found = []
        for line in lines:
            for k in LATER_KEYS:
                line = line.replace(k, "")
            found.append(line)
        return found

    kept, rerun = keys(kept), keys(rerun)
    for prefix in LATER_LINES:
        if not any(line.startswith(prefix) for line in kept):
            rerun = [line for line in rerun if not line.startswith(prefix)]
    return kept, rerun


pairs = [("p0_harness_check.txt", "out_rerun/p0_harness_check.txt")]
pairs += [(f"out/{f}", f"out_rerun/{f}") for f in sorted(os.listdir("out")) if f.endswith(".txt")]
tally = {"IDENTICAL": 0, "DIFFERS": 0, "MISSING": 0, "NOT RERUN": 0}
later_same = 0
for kept_path, rerun_path in pairs:
    name = os.path.basename(kept_path)
    if kept_path in NOT_RERUN:
        print(f"NOT RERUN  {name}: {NOT_RERUN[kept_path]}")
        tally["NOT RERUN"] += 1
        continue
    if not os.path.exists(rerun_path):
        print(f"MISSING    {name}: no {rerun_path}")
        tally["MISSING"] += 1
        continue
    kept, rerun = without_times(read(kept_path)), without_times(read(rerun_path))
    if kept == rerun:
        print(f"IDENTICAL  {name}")
        tally["IDENTICAL"] += 1
        continue
    tally["DIFFERS"] += 1
    k2, r2 = without_later(kept, rerun)
    if k2 == r2:
        later_same += 1
        note = "the same once the later defaults and lines are dropped"
    else:
        note = "still differs once the later defaults and lines are dropped"
    print(f"DIFFERS    {name} ({note})")
    shown = list(difflib.unified_diff(kept, rerun, "kept", "rerun", n=0, lineterm=""))[2:10]
    for line in shown:
        print("             " + line[:200])
print(f"compared {len(pairs)}: " + ", ".join(f"{k} {v}" for k, v in tally.items())
      + f"; of the DIFFERS, {later_same} the same once the later defaults and lines are dropped")
EOF

# Draft "triggers": law 4's top and bottom from the clock, and the many bangs

For David (Tensor24's derivations), by a helper. Under the protocol (TENSOR24.md section 19): steps 1 to 4 and a self-check of step 6 are here; step 5 (the second hand), Sofer's law check and David's sign-off are not mine.

- **Base:** the head's engine at toys-5 b56643b0e (its src is engine-5.0-test's, last changed at 834cfd72e), imported from the repository with `/home/user/Universe24/venv314/bin/python -I`. Every variant is a function assigned at run time (t24h.py); no file of the repository is edited. The harness reads the head bit for bit (p0: 11 of 11, below).
- **Read:** the plan at engine-5.0-test 33d9ecaa3 (laws 4 and 10, sections 16, 23 and 30), its newer 08ec59132 through the GitHub API ("no bound and no floor", section 30 item 10), the law at scribe-5 f32b67cc3 (docs/ALGEBRA.md lines 132, 1608, 1610 and 1909), drafts C and B with their verifiers, and #2187 to 12:14 UTC (Animate's 6468340318 and 6468562956; the Boss's 6468487676 and 6468668551, law 11 "every bound is a clock's carry").
- **Marks:** every claim is **PROVED** (with its proof), **CHECKED** (with its probe and count) or **OPEN**.

## 0. The answers

**(a) "The rate 0 gives nothing."**
- **The one formula as it stands does not give it** (PROVED; CHECKED). At a clock whose Link rate reads 0, the grips are 0. The clock's own inertia, its spring and the push are not 0, so the time gear coasts: X_step - X1 is 517,114,983 at 5,000,000 quanta and 160,335,814 at 500,000.
- **Formula.** Weight the time gear's step by its clock's own tick count, n = (p_m + Gamma - 1) div Gamma: Rule3 on a dial one tooth short of its turn, 1 at every Link rate from 1 to Gamma and 0 at 0. With it the stopped clock's time gear does not step: X2 = X1.
- **The top comes by itself** (PROVED). A run from a live setting can reach the stop only rising (X0 < X1, theorem A1), so law 4's own weak maximum turns every stopped clock at its stopped tick. No ceiling is typed: X_c = 4,268,834,097 is the rate chain's own zero.
- **BH-S1's restore is replaced** (PROVED; CHECKED). The turnover rewrites every gear of a turned clock from its held state, so the restore's write is dead on every such run.
  - Bit for bit: 251 of 251 states at 5,000,000 quanta, 301 of 301 at 500,000, 901 of 901 on the hole at 900 ticks.
  - Stopped clock-ticks: 320, 128 and 397, every one rising and turned.
  - The rewind is 20 of 20 through the top.
  - 6 of 6 mutants are caught.
- **What the head does instead:** it computes the stopped clock's whole step, writes every gear back to its held value, and law 4 reads the written-back gear (FR-S1). On every run here the write-back is overwritten by the turnover in the same tick.
- **What stays, OPEN:** n is BH-S1's own stopped mask read as a coefficient. Its other uses stay as n's products: the wall (striking it moves the hole from tick 476), the dial's rest, the click's skips and the rewind note's frozen mask. Whether n, an indicator spelt as Rule3's quotient, passes "no bound and no floor" is Sofer's to read.

**(b) The bottom without a typed floor.** The one formula gives the full rate. It does not give the weak minimum.
- **The weak minimum is REFUTED** (PROVED; CHECKED). Beside the weak maximum, it holds every time gear at rest for ever: a clock with X0 = X1 turns the first tick it moves either way, and the turnover returns it to rest.
  - Every world starts at rest, so every world freezes at tick 1. All six charts, the node and the threshold route turn every clock at every tick, with 0 births.
  - It contradicts law 4's own sentence: "The one big bang's rest at the full rate is neither, and does not turn".
- **The strict minimum** (both strict; not the mirror) lets the hole's fall pass through X = 0 within a tick, as no bottom trigger at all does: refused at tick 6.
- **The full rate is the step's own edge** (PROVED; CHECKED). The light's checkerboard step is a+ + a- = (2 - 4 (p_m / Gamma)^2) a. It is bounded for p_m < Gamma, has a double root at p_m = Gamma (the vacuum, linear growth), and grows exponentially above. The rates' table puts p_m <= Gamma exactly at the loads c >= 0, so X >= Gamma is the stable side: X_f = Gamma is derived.
- **As law 4's second trigger** (CHECKED), X_f = Gamma keeps every slot at or above Gamma on every chart. F5's clamp and the typed hill depth (stretch.LOW = -1) then never act. The typed X_f = 19 acts only on the hole (160 turned clock-ticks), where without any floor X reaches 0 by tick 6.
- **Under the prohibition:** whether a comparison with the calibre's full rate is "a floor value" is Sofer's and the owner's. The probes say the one formula has no bottom without one.

**(c) The many bangs.**
- **The 00-equation route (R) does not swing between the two ends** (CHECKED). On the bang's calibre with the heavy sea it swings every 13 ticks between X of about Gamma and 1.15 Gamma, far from the stop, so (a) never acts.
- **R's differing tops are not "more"** (PROVED; CHECKED). Its bottoms drift down and its rewind fails (0 of 20), both from one cause: the velocity term X'^2 / 2X read at the tick's start breaks T S T = S^-1 (1,216 of 4,864 integers after T S^4 T).
- **Written exactly in the Link's length L** (X = L L div Gamma, where the 00 equation is a pure brake read at the start):
  - T S^4 T = S^-4 holds to the last integer (4,864 of 4,864);
  - the rewind is 20 of 20 through the turnovers;
  - every swing repeats the one before (29 of 29 on 4^3; one course for all 30 swings on 5^3);
  - every gear returns every two swings; only the dial and the click counter do not.
- **Draft C's threshold route (V) with (a) does swing between the two ends** (CHECKED). Its top is the Link rate's zero, and its bottom the full rate at rest, where the push turns the course out with no trigger. Its rewind is exact through the top.
- **But no later bang shows more** (CHECKED).
  - At 5,000,000 quanta the births run 0, 64, 32, 0, 0 a swing, and the rise after the fifth bottom repeats the third's tick for tick.
  - At 500,000 quanta the fittings run 0, 32, 0, 32: the still chimes rise (832, 9,280, 10,304) while the kinds alternate.
- **The head's turnover is not the exact time reversal** (CHECKED). The regulator's three flow gears are odd under T: their source negates exactly at the retracing step (192 of 192). The turnover reverses them as even gears, so their drive fine teeth miss by 2N (192 of 192).
  - With the flows odd under T (K = -1 on them, as on a hand's twelve gear), T S^m T = S^-m holds to the last integer on V, on the head's own bang and on RL (4,864 of 4,864 each). The rewind stays 20 of 20.
- **With that exact time reversal every A1 bang is a copy** (CHECKED).
  - On the threshold route: births 0 at 5,000,000 quanta (against 96) and 0 at 500,000 (against 64, and no still chime); every swing's course equals the one before, and all tops are one number.
  - On the head's own bang: births 0 (against 64).
  - So every click measured after the first top on these courses came from the turnover's flaw. On RL's sea, where T S T is exact with the head's turnover already (no flows), the same still chimes click again every two swings.
  - On the structured hole and nature, whose clocks turn at different ticks, the clicks stay: births 30 against 28, and 2 against 3.
- **Why** (PROVED). Where T S T = S^-1, a whole-board turnover makes the next swing retrace the last. A bang can show more only where the board leaves the one clock (local turnovers) or a click breaks the time reversal and does not die out. Rows 46 and 1c: OPEN on every route measured.

## 1. The law as it stands (quoted, scribe-5 f32b67cc3)

- **Line 132, BH-S1:** "at a Node-clock whose mesh rate reads 0 (the frozen load) no act changes its state: every gear restored to the held gear after the step (now, before, the remainder, the teeth count), its proper clock unchanged, its six Meshes at the teeth count (the factor 0 where either end is frozen, the mask read from the start load and from no file key), the frozen mask booked beside the turned mask in the frame so the rewind restores the same".
- **Line 132, FR-S1:** "the frozen Node is turned at its frozen interval by the swap's own trigger on the restored line (X2 = X1 and X0 < X1 at every Node that froze by the dynamics)".
- **Line 1608, The floor:** "the floor is the least volume with a reading, X_f = stretch_of(Gamma, -Gamma), the volume whose notch reads -Gamma, the hill one Gamma deep (... 19 at Gamma 1,000 ...)".
  - "The trigger is one symmetric function of the Node-clock's three notches as the top's is, an outer notch below the floor, X0 < X_f or X2 < X_f".
- **Line 1909, bound (2):** "no Node-clock runs faster than the full rate: a load below 0 (a hill) is read at 0 for the rate and the Mesh rate, the load itself, the stretch and the turnover's bottom untouched".
  - "The edge under (1) and (2) is Rule3's own (David: the conserved form is an energy while D plus twice the grips summed is at most 4 w at every Node-clock, the vacuum's light standing on it exactly ...)".
- **The plan, law 4:** "the time gear's weak maximum (X0 <= X1 >= X2 with one of the two strict: the top), or its output falling below the floor X_f ... The one big bang's rest at the full rate is neither, and does not turn (Sofer)".
- **The plan, section 30, item 10 (08ec59132):** "the candidate from the symmetries is the bottom as the top's mirror, the time gear's weak minimum (X0 >= X1 <= X2, one strict), with no floor value".

## 2. (a) The rate 0 gives nothing

### 2.1 What the one formula gives at the Link rate 0 (PROVED; CHECKED)

**The regulator's turn** (law 2; clock.turn, tick.carry):

    w X2' + r' = (2w - D_R - SUM_j R_j) X1 - w X0 + SUM_j R_j X_j + r,   then X2 = X2' + q (the drive)

- R_j = 2 pinion p_m^2 Q_j G^2 are the grips. At p_m = 0 all six are 0, with no mask (draft B's zero).
- What is left is X2 = 2 X1 - X0 + floor((r - D_R X1) / w) + q. Here D_R = 12 (rim - pinion_R) p_0^2 G^2 is read at the clock's rate p_0 (22 at the stopped load 3,793; at the held load, with the held field, no more), not at p_m, so it is not 0. Draft C's courses set it to 0 (its item (c)); the coasting and the push remain.
- **The driven gears:** p+ = p + X F + kick, with F = -D a + J once the grips are 0, and a+ = a + (p+ + mid W+) div W+. They coast and spring.
- So the one formula gives nothing on the Links alone.

**CHECKED** (p1): the head's discarded time-gear step at its stopped clocks, X_step - X1, is above 0 at every one:
- least 517,114,983 at 5,000,000 quanta;
- least 160,335,814 at 500,000;
- least 156,122,331 on the hole.

### 2.2 The formula, in the three operations

    n = (p_m + Gamma - 1) div Gamma                 Rule3: p_m + (Gamma - 1) = n Gamma + r'
    time gear out = held + n (stepped - held)        on its now, before, fine tooth and drive fine tooth

- **In clock words:** the Node-clock's own tick count at its Link rate, its dial standing one tooth short of a turn. A Link rate of one tooth or more turns it once; none turns it not at all. Its remainder is no notch: it is a coefficient of the tick, as p_m = rounded(p_0^2, Gamma) itself is (rates.rounded keeps no fine tooth).
- **PROVED:** for 1 <= p_m <= Gamma, Gamma <= p_m + Gamma - 1 <= 2 Gamma - 1, so n = 1. At p_m = 0, n = 0. So at every live clock (a) is the head's step, and at a stopped clock the time gear does not step: X2 = X1.

### 2.3 The top by itself, and the restore replaced (PROVED)

**Definitions.**
- Let S = {X : X >= X_c} be the stopped set, with X_c = stretch_of(Gamma, 3,793) = 4,268,834,097.
- load_of is non-decreasing in X, and p_m is non-increasing in the load, so p_m(load_of(X)) = 0 if and only if X is in S.

**Theorem A1** (a stopped clock is always rising into the stop). Assume the setting holds every time gear below X_c, and only the step and the turnover write the time gear (no click does: the recoil drives the flow gears, the release the unsprung hand). Then at every tick that starts with now in S at a clock, its before is not in S, so X0 < X_c <= X1.

*Proof* by the invariant I(t): at the start of tick t, now in S implies before not in S.
- **Base:** at t = 1 no now is in S.
- **Step, now in S:** by I(t), X0 < X1. Under (a), X2 = X1, so law 4's weak maximum (X0 <= X1 >= X2, X0 < X1 strict) turns the clock. The turnover sets now(t+1) = X0, which is not in S.
- **Step, now not in S, turned:** now(t+1) = X0(t) and before(t+1) = X1(t), which is not in S. I(t+1) holds whatever X0 is.
- **Step, now not in S, unturned:** before(t+1) = X1(t), which is not in S. QED.

**Corollary A2** (the top with no ceiling). Under (a) every stopped clock turns at its stopped tick, by law 4's weak maximum on X2 = X1. No ceiling is typed: X_c is the least stretch whose Link rate rounds to 0. This is R9 in part: the top is the weak maximum of a time gear whose step is weighted by a carry, n.

**Corollary A3** (the restore replaced).
- clock.reverse sets every train of a turned clock to its held train, exchanged and mirrored. So whatever the step or a restore wrote at that clock in the tick is overwritten.
- By A1 no stopped clock is left unturned. So BH-S1's restore at the stopped clocks that are not turned (tick.py:285-287) is never reached.
- Its only effect on a run is the trigger's X2 = X1, which (a) gives.
- Backward, the head returns a turned clock to the turnover's kept pre-state (tick.py:281-284). That belongs to the turnover's undoing, not to BH-S1.

**The same holds on the head:** BH-S1 plus FR-S1 is A1 with the restore in place of (a).

### 2.4 CHECKED: (a) in place of the restore, and the head as it is (p1, course.py)

| the hole, the head's own drive (the head alone; its mutant below) | 560 | 12 | 12 | 12 | | | |
| the hole, the head's own drive | 900 | 397 | 397 | 397 | 901 of 901 | | |

The tops are at ticks 24, 71, 118, 165 and 212 at 5,000,000 quanta (draft C's own, its state hash 77e8684641d42469 at 250 ticks reproduced), and 74, 221, 368 and 515 at 500,000 (draft C's two, and two more in 600 ticks).

**Mutants**, each a different state from the head (caught 6 of 6):

| mutant | case | result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| n read from the clock's rate p_0 instead of the Link rate (p_0 = 22, so n = 1) | 5,000,000 | 227 of 251 differ from tick 24; no top; 14,528 stopped clock-ticks never turned |

| n read from p_0 | the hole | 85 of 561 differ from tick 476; 159 never turned |

So the time gear's hold is the load-bearing part of BH-S1, and the restore of the other gears is not.

### 2.5 What the head does instead (CHECKED; tick.py, readings.py, meshes.py, click.py)

At a clock whose Link rate reads 0 at the tick's start:
1. readings.stopped_of builds the mask from mesh_rate == 0.
2. The dial rests: where(stopped, 0, rate), while the rate is 22, not 0.
3. Its six Meshes' factors are set to 0 at both ends (the wall).
4. The whole step is computed: the time gear coasts, the hands coast and spring, and the release and the recoil act.
5. Every gear is written back to its held value: BH-S1's restore at stopped clocks that are not turned.
6. Law 4 reads the written-back gear, X0 < X1 = X2, and turns the clock (FR-S1).
7. The turnover overwrites step 5 at that clock.

The clicks skip the clock too: the engagement and the far wind & ~stopped (click.py:65 and 73), the bobs (bobs.py:51) and the split (bobs.py:160). The rewind note books the frozen mask (run.py:156).

### 2.6 What stays of BH-S1, and the rate 0 in the course (OPEN; PROVED)

**What stays.**
- n is BH-S1's own mask read as a coefficient. Its other uses stay, now as n's products, bit for bit the masks since n is 0 or 1:
  - the wall: the factor times n_i n_j;
  - the dial: plus n p_0;
  - the click's skips;
  - the rewind note's frozen mask.
- **The wall struck** (CHECKED): the hole at 900 ticks moves from tick 476, the first stop. 425 of 901 states differ; there are 4,021 turns against 4,020 and 395 stopped clock-ticks against 397; births are 35 both. A wall from the formula would need the grip read from both ends' rates (for example 2 pinion p_m(i) p_m(j) Q G^2, symmetric). That changes every non-uniform board: OPEN, not run.

**Sofer's reading** (OPEN): n is an indicator spelt as Rule3's quotient.
- It holds no integer of the state in a range: it weighs the time gear's step by its clock's own tick count.
- It is the stopped mask as a product. Under "no clamp (... an indicator quotient read as a cap)", that is his to read.

**The form with the remainder kept** (OPEN): the dial f + p_m = n Gamma + f', the owner's asynchronous board.
- It gives n = 0 for ever at p_m = 0 with nothing dropped (PROVED: Rule3 with input 0 carries nothing).
- But at 0 < p_m < Gamma the clock skips great ticks, so the step's coefficients (W, s, the grips) must lose the rate they carry now.
- Law 4 must then be read at the clock's own ticks, and the stop at its last live tick (Animate's words). With the stop read from the output's rate, that is the ceiling X2 >= X_c under another name.

**Theorem A4** (the stop belongs to the carry, not to the course) (PROVED).
- In the continuum, the 00 equation is a pure brake only in Y = a^2 (theorem C1). Any other variable Z = g(Y) gains the velocity term g''(Y) Y'^2.
- Y' = 2 a H does not vanish as the rate p_0 = Gamma / a goes to 0.
- So no variable of the time gear both steps exactly (an explicit leapfrog read at the start) and stops at the rate 0.
- The stop is the lattice's Link rate rounding to 0 (p_0 = 22, not 0: the physicist's artifact, section 30). "The rate 0 gives nothing" is a law of the clock's tick count, not a property of the course.

## 3. (b) The bottom without a typed floor

### 3.1 The weak minimum (REFUTED: PROVED; CHECKED)

**Theorem B1.** With the weak minimum (X0 >= X1 <= X2, one strict) beside the weak maximum, a clock whose time gear is at rest (X0 = X1) never changes it.

*Proof:*
- If X2 > X1 the minimum fires; if X2 < X1 the maximum fires. Either way the turnover ends the tick at the held state exchanged, (X1, X0) = (X0, X0): rest again.
- If X2 = X1 the gear is still at rest.
- By induction on ticks, X = X1 for ever. Each turned tick reverses the hands, so they swing in a two-cycle. QED.

**The consequences:**
- Every world starts at rest: clock.start_state sets the time gear to the full rate in both slots. So under B1 no world moves.
- It contradicts law 4's "The one big bang's rest at the full rate is neither, and does not turn".
- At the swing's bottom (X back at 1,000 at rest) it would bounce the course back at its arrival instead of letting the push turn it out.

**CHECKED** (p2): every clock turned at every tick from tick 1, births 0, on every chart.

| world | ticks | turns (= clocks x ticks) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| the hole | 60 | 7,500 = 125 x 60 |
| nature | 60 | 7,500 |
| the chiral | 60 | 7,500 |
| the node | 20 | 540 = 27 x 20 |
| bodies | 120 | 41,160 = 343 x 120 |
| the bang | 250 | 16,000 = 64 x 250 |
| ga2 | 230 | 14,720 = 64 x 230 |
| the threshold route, 5,000,000 quanta | 250 | 250 whole-board turnovers |

**The strict minimum, tried as a repair** (CHECKED). X0 > X1 < X2 is not the top's mirror. It does not freeze a rest.
- On the hole it is refused at tick 6: "the volume X is at most 0", least X -2,211. With no bottom trigger at all, the hole is refused at tick 6 too, least X -593.
- Elsewhere it adds bottom turns and keeps the hills:

| world | bottom turns | clock-ticks below Gamma |
| --- | --- | --- |
| nature | 1,629 | 1,659 |
| the chiral | 2,443 | 328 |
| the node | 59 | 71 |
| bodies | 2,964 | 80 |
| ga2 | 64 | 64 |
| the bang | 0 (bit for bit) | 0 |

**Theorem B2.** No trigger on the shape of the three notches keeps X inside the reading's domain. A fall from X1 > 0 to X2 <= 0 within one tick is no minimum (X0 >= X1 > X2). So some comparison of the output X2 with a value is needed: the form of law 4's second trigger. (PROVED.)

### 3.2 The full rate, the one formula's own edge (PROVED; CHECKED)

**Theorem B3.**
- For the light (pinion = rim, D = 0) at no tension, (D + 2 SUM_j R_j) / w = 24 rim p_m^2 G^2 / (6 rim Gamma^2 G^2) = 4 (p_m / Gamma)^2.
- So the checkerboard (k = pi on every axis, a_j = -a) steps as a+ + a- = (2 - 4 (p_m / Gamma)^2) a, that is cos omega = 1 - 2 (p_m / Gamma)^2.
- |cos omega| <= 1 if and only if p_m <= Gamma. At p_m = Gamma the double root -1 gives a+ = -2 a - a- exactly (the vacuum on the edge: linear growth). Above it the growth is exponential.
- A train with a spring is strictly inside at the full rate: D + 2 SUM R = 12 G^2 Gamma^2 (rim + pinion) <= 24 rim Gamma^2 G^2, with equality only at pinion = rim.
- The rates' table puts p_m <= Gamma at every load c >= 0 and p_m > Gamma at every hill.
- load_of(X) >= 0 if and only if X >= stretch_of(0) = Gamma.
- So the least stretch at which the step is stable is X = Gamma: X_f = Gamma derived. This is the law's own "D plus twice the grips summed is at most 4 w" (line 1909) read at the turnover, instead of as F5's clamp.

**CHECKED** (p3_edge.py), on the head's own rule3.gearing and acts.carry over 2,000 ticks: 8,702 cases, 0 failures.
- Bounded at every p_m from 950 to 999.
- Exactly |a| = 1,999 (k + 1) - 1,000 at p_m = 1,000.
- Beyond 10^9 at every p_m from 1,001 to 1,050.
- The rates' table: p_m <= Gamma at 7,601 loads c >= 0, and p_m > Gamma at 1,000 hills.
- Mutants caught, 4 of 4: the grips' sign flipped, the grips' factor 2 dropped, the edge read at Gamma - 1, and the light given a spring.
- A fifth mutant, the carry's fine tooth reset each tick, moves no edge and is not caught (out/p3_edge_nofine.txt): the edge is the linear map's, not the rounding's.

**As law 4's second trigger** (X0 < Gamma or X2 < Gamma, in place of 19) (PROVED):
- No time-gear slot ever falls below Gamma: an unturned clock's new now is X2 >= Gamma, a turned clock's is X0 >= Gamma by induction from the start at Gamma, and the before slots are earlier nows.
- So the start's load is never below 0, and F5's clamp (readings.rates_of, max(c, 0)) and the typed hill depth (stretch.LOW = -1) never act.
- CHECKED: 0 clock-ticks below Gamma on all seven charts.

### 3.3 Which one the one formula gives

The full rate.
- It is the step's own edge (B3), and it keeps the stretch inside the reading's domain (B2).
- The weak minimum is no symmetry the formula needs. T maps a weak maximum to a weak maximum (X0 and X2 exchanged). No act of the step exchanges the Link rate's two ends, Gamma and 0, so nothing maps the top to the bottom (a reading of the step, not a theorem). And it freezes every rest (B1).
- **Under the prohibition** (OPEN, Sofer and the owner): X_f = Gamma is a comparison with the calibre's full rate. It is no typed number, but it is a bound. Law 11's reading: X2 < Gamma means the output's rate is above the great clock's, so the clock's dial would carry more than one tick in a great tick; its event is the turnover.

### 3.4 What moves on the head's worlds against the typed floor (CHECKED, p2_bottom.py; the head's own drive and acts)

| world, ticks | the head, X_f = 19: turns (by the floor) / births / least X / clock-ticks below Gamma | X_f = Gamma: states differing (first) / turns (by the floor) / births / least X | no bottom trigger |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| hole, 60 | 1,073 (160) / 28 / 34 / 220 | 57 of 61 (tick 4) / 997 (153) / 30 / 1,000 | refused at tick 6 (X <= 0; least -593) |
| nature, 60 | 861 (0) / 3 / 814 / 185 | 58 of 61 (3) / 997 (41) / 2 / 1,000 | the head bit for bit |
| chiral (nature at 5 quanta), 60 | 635 (0) / 5 / 829 / 50 | 58 of 61 (3) / 694 (15) / 6 / 1,000 | bit for bit |
| node, 20 | 117 (0) / 0 / 831 / 61 | 18 of 21 (3) / 135 (17) / 0 / 1,000 | bit for bit |
| bodies, 120 | 1,934 (0) / 0 / 957 / 16 | 111 of 121 (10) / 2,046 (8) / 0 / 1,000 | bit for bit |
| bang, 250 | 3,200 (0) / 64 / 1,000 / 0 | 0 of 251 (bit for bit) | bit for bit |
| ga2, 230 | 64 (0) / 32 / 998 / 64 | 35 of 231 (196) / 96 (32) / 48 / 1,008 | bit for bit |
| threshold route, 5,000,000 quanta, 250 | 320 (0) / 96 / 1,000 / 0 | 0 of 251 (bit for bit) | |

So:
- The typed floor is load-bearing on the hole alone. On the other five charts it never fires, and removing it changes nothing.
- The full rate moves the five structured charts from tick 3 to 10 (196 on ga2): it turns their local hills over instead of clamping their rates.
- It leaves the bang and the threshold route bit for bit: their bottom is X = 1,000 at rest, never below.

## 4. (c) The many bangs

### 4.1 The three courses, in the three operations (PROVED: the forms)

**V, draft C's derived push** (its section 4): q = carry(X ((d+1) G + 2d V), Gamma key, r_d).
- G = carry(SUM (-a pull - D a a), T) is the Meshes' wind, and V = carry(SUM D a a, T) the springs'.

**R, draft C's verifier's 00 equation in X:** q = carry(X'^2 / 2X - X ((d+1) K - (d-1) V) / (Gamma key)).
- K = SUM u_in u_out (the kinetic products), and the velocity term carry(Gamma key (X - X_other)^2, X + X) is read at the tick's start.
- 'before' reads X- in both goings (the verifier's). 'going' reads X- forward and X+ backward.

**RL, the same 00 equation in the Link's length L** (new). The time gear holds L, and the engine reads X = L L div Gamma (mean teeth, load, stretch, triggers):

    q_L = carry(-L ((d+1) K - (d-1) V), 2 Gamma key, r_d)        (numerator times trace rim Gamma^3 / 2 over the drive teeth)

**Theorem C1** (PROVED).
- With X = L^2 / Gamma: X' = 2 L L' / Gamma and X'' = 2 (L'^2 + L L'') / Gamma, while X'^2 / 2X = 2 L'^2 / Gamma.
- So X'' - X'^2 / 2X = 2 L L'' / Gamma exactly, and (R) is the same as L'' = -L ((d+1) K - (d-1) V) / (2 Gamma key): a pure brake with no velocity term.
- L is the Link's length in vacuum teeth: X = Gamma^5 / p_0^4 and p_m = p_0^2 / Gamma give L = Gamma^2 / p_m = Gamma a^2, "a Link's length is the other face of its clock's rate". L = Gamma at the vacuum, so the settings are unchanged.

**Theorem C2** (PROVED).
- RL's numerator reads only the tick's start: L, K and V. K = SUM u_in u_out is unchanged when the in and out strides swap, which is what the rewind does.
- So the time gear's act is the head's regulator act, Rule3's turn and one drive carry, and its rewind is law 3's.
- R's velocity term reads (X - X-)^2 forward and (X+ - X)^2 backward. Those are equal only where X'' = 0, so R's rewind fails.

### 4.2 Does the board swing between its two ends by itself? (CHECKED, course.py)

**R and RL, the 00 equation** (the bang's calibre, the heavy row 2 laid at rest at every Node-clock, sense 1): **no.**

| quanta | board | course | swing |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 20 | 4^3 and 5^3 | tops every 13 ticks (31 in 400) | X 942..1,152 (R), 1,000..1,153 (RL) |
| 2 | | period 49 | X 1,000..1,196 |
| 200 | | period 3 | X 1,000..1,243 |
| 2,000 | | period 11 | X 1,000..13,741 |
| 20, with the bang's light at 500 | | a three-tick flicker | X 1,000..1,038 |

- No stopped clock-tick occurs, so (a) never acts. The stop X_c = 4,268,834,097 is about 3 x 10^5 times above every top.
- The course turns where the matter's rho + 3p changes sign (2K against V, as the field oscillates). The swing is set by the matter's spring and the held field, not by the two ends of the rate.
- (Draft C's verifier's sea, with its calibre of one heavy row, reached X of about 6,900: the held field of the bang's calibre shortens the swing.)

**V, the threshold route, with (a): yes.**
- **The top:** the Link rate's zero, every clock stopped and turned at once.
- **The bottom:** the full rate at rest (X = 1,000 two ticks running), where the push turns the course out with no trigger.
- 5 bangs in 250 ticks at 5,000,000 quanta (tops 24, 71, 118, 165 and 212); 4 in 600 at 500,000 (74, 221, 368 and 515).

### 4.3 Is the rewind exact across a turnover? T S T = S^-1? (CHECKED, course.py and p4_tst.py)

**The rewind, 20 ticks forward and back through whole-board turnovers:**

| course | rewind | through |
| --- | --- | --- |
| R, the sea | 0 of 20 | turnovers at 33 and 46 |
| R, sea and light | 11 of 20 | |
| RL, the sea | 20 of 20 | 33 and 46, on 4^3 and 5^3 |
| RL, sea and light | 20 of 20 | 32 to 50 |
| V with (a), 5,000,000 quanta | 20 of 20 | 24 |
| V with (a), 500,000 quanta | 20 of 20 | 74 |

**T S^m T against S^-m**, every integer of every gear (4,864 on 4^3):

| course | integers equal | where the others are |
| --- | --- | --- |
| RL, the sea, m = 4 | 4,864 of 4,864 | |
| R, the sea, either velocity reading | 3,648 | 1,216 off: the time gear, and the fine teeth of every hand, the held field and the offset, at every clock |
| V, 5,000,000, m = 5 | 4,672 | 192 off: the regulator's three flow gears' drive fine teeth |
| the head's bang, m = 1 | 4,480 | 384 off: the flows' before slots and drive fine teeth |
| mutant: T without the one mirror, on RL | 3,264 (caught) | |

- With the strides' odd rounding read at 2P instead of 2P + 1, the same 192 and 384 integers differ. But the + 1 changes the rounding only where -2P lands exactly on a half of d = 2 T X, which these runs never meet (the hole is bit for bit with it over 30 ticks; out/p4_uround_live.txt). So the check rules that rounding out only on these runs.
- **The flows' cause** (CHECKED, p4b and p4c): the turnover's sign on the flow gears, not their source.
  - The flows' drive numerators (the stress's flow parts times p_0^2) are odd under T exactly: N' = -N_t at 192 of 192 (3 flows at 64 clocks) at the step that retraces x_(t-1) to x_t. The time gear's push is even: N' = +N_t at 64 of 64.
  - The flows' drive fine teeth are never 0 there (p4b), so this is not the mirror's failing case at an even count (DAVID-TENSOR24-ANY-TEETH.md).
  - The head's turnover mirrors every drive fine tooth once (clock.py:232), which is the reversal of an even source. For an odd source the retracing step adds -N to 2 mid - (r + N) and misses 2 mid - r by 2N (192 of 192).

**PROVED** (the drive's carry under T). A drive (q, r') = carry(N + r) adds q in the second difference (gear.now advanced after the turn).
- If the source is even (N' = N), the retrace needs (q, m(r)) from N + m(r'). Since N = q T_d + r' - r, this holds if and only if m(r) - m(r') = r' - r, that is m(r) = c - r: the mirror.
- If the source is odd (N' = -N), the gear itself must be odd (its slots exchanged and negated, K = -1) for the second difference to retrace (then q'' = -q). That needs -q from -N + m(r'), which holds if and only if m(r) - r = m(r') - r', that is m the identity: the drive fine tooth mirrored twice, once for the exchange and once for the negation.
- The rule is acts.reverse's own for a negated gear's main fine tooth (a hand's twelve gear: mirrored twice, kept). The main fine tooth of the negated flow gears under that rule is CHECKED by p4 below, not proved here.

**CHECKED** (p4_tst.py flows=odd, and the engine switch tflows=odd): with K = -1 on the three flow gears and every fine tooth and drive fine tooth mirrored once per reversal of its gear:
- T S^m T = S^-m on V at 5,000,000 quanta (m = 5), on the head's bang (m = 1) and on RL (m = 4): 4,864 of 4,864 each;
- the rewind 20 of 20 through the tops on V at both energies and on the head's bang;
- the hole and nature 20 of 20 from tick 20.
- A reading, not a proof: the flows stand for the metric's g_0i (the momentum density), which time reversal negates in general relativity.

### 4.4 Does each bang show more (row 46)? Is no bang a copy (row 1c)? (CHECKED)

**R, the sea (20):**
- The clicks are still chimes only, alternating 448 and 384 a swing, with 0 births. The kinds and counts do not rise.
- No swing repeats the one before, but the difference is R's velocity term: its bottoms drift 999, 998, 996, ... 942, under the full rate (8,000 clock-ticks below Gamma), and its rewind is 0 of 20.

**RL, the sea:**
- The same still chimes, 448 and 384.
- Every swing's X course equals the one before (29 of 29 on 4^3). On 5^3 all 30 swings have one course (435 pairs).
- **p5:** two swings apart (tick 30 against 56), every gear of every train and the bobs' state are equal. One swing apart the hands are the time-reversed images. Only the dial and the click counter never return (64 of 64 differ): they count up through every turnover.
- Each bang is a copy.

**V with (a), 5,000,000 quanta:**
- Births run 0, 64, 32, 0, 0 in the five swings to tick 212, and 0 after; there are no other clicks.
- The tops are 4,638,548,302, 4,639,582,178, 4,641,211,062, 4,641,121,200 and 4,641,211,062.
- The rise after the fifth bottom (189..212) equals the third's (95..118) at all 24 ticks. No whole swing pair is equal: the descents retrace different rises.

**V with (a), 500,000 quanta:**
- Fittings run 0, 32, 0, 32 a swing, and still chimes 0, 832, 9,280 and 10,304 (and 10,752 in the 85 ticks of the fifth).
- The tops rise: 4,393,346,240, 4,396,644,616, 4,397,595,723 and 4,399,938,656. No pair of swings is equal.
- The counts rise, the kinds alternate.

**The same courses with the exact time reversal** (the flows odd under T; course.py tflows=odd):

| course | births | clicks | swings | tops | rewind |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| V, 5,000,000, 250 ticks | 0 (against 96) | none | all 4 complete swings one course (6 pairs) | all 4,638,548,302 | 20 of 20 |
| V, 500,000, 600 ticks | 0 (against 64) | none (against 31,168 still chimes) | 3 pairs equal | all 4,393,346,240 | 20 of 20 |
| the head's bang, 100 ticks | 0 (against 64 at ticks 6 and 7) | | every swing equal to the one before (against period two) | | 20 of 20, and 98 of 101 states apart from the head's from tick 3 |
| the hole, 60 ticks | 30 (against 28) | | | | 20 of 20; 58 of 61 states apart from tick 3 |
| nature, 60 ticks | 2 (against 3) | | | | 20 of 20; 59 of 61 states apart from tick 2 |

So on the one clock (A1) the exact time reversal leaves nothing to show: each bang is the last one's copy. The clicks of the A1 courses above came from the turnover's flaw. On the structured worlds, whose clocks turn at different ticks, the board as a whole is never reversed at once, and the clicks stay.

### 4.5 Why (PROVED)

**Theorem C3.**
- If T S T = S^-1 on the whole state, clicks included, then S^m T x = T S^-m x.
- A whole-board turnover at tick n ends it at T x_(n-1). Then S^m T x_(n-1) = T x_(n-1-m): the board runs back through the reversed states of the run before, to T x_0 (the seed reversed), then on as the mirror of the first swing.
- So every swing is the time-reversed copy of the one before, and nothing the clicks wrote is new: rows 1c and 46 fail.

**Consequences.**
- A bang can differ from the last only through an act that breaks T S T = S^-1 while S stays a bijection, so that the rewind stays exact.
- On this engine that act was:
  - the turnover's even sign on the odd flow gears (a flaw: with it corrected the A1 courses click no more);
  - in R, the velocity term (an artifact that also breaks the rewind).
- With the time reversal exact, the A1 bangs are copies (4.4).
- Law 10's two halves, the circle and "every bang shows more", meet only where the board leaves the one clock (local turnovers at different ticks, as on the structured worlds), or a click breaks the time reversal bang after bang. Which, and why it does not die out, is OPEN.

## 5. What it replaces and what it changes

**What it replaces:**
- **R10 / BH-S1's restore** (line 132; tick.py:285-287): by (a), the time gear's step times n. The state is bit for bit (sections 2.4 and 2.3). The wall, the dial's rest, the click's skips and the note's frozen mask stay as n's products (OPEN).
- **FR-S1's trigger** "on the restored line" becomes law 4's own weak maximum on X2 = X1, the same trigger with no restore under it.
- **R9 in part:** the top needs no ceiling.
- **Law 4's second trigger** (line 1608): X_f = 19 and the typed hill depth (stretch.LOW = -1) become X_f = Gamma, the step's edge. With it F5's clamp (line 1909, bound (2)) never acts. It moves the five structured charts (the table in 3.4).
- **The weak minimum** (section 30, item 10): REFUTED as the bottom.
- **Draft C's 00-equation route:** its "each top different, no declared number" is REFUTED as "every bang shows more": that is the velocity term's asymmetry, the same that breaks its rewind. Its exact form RL is given, its rewind exact, its swings copies.
- **Law 4's mirror** (clock.reverse; acts.reverse with hands.signs): the regulator's three flow gears to be odd under T (K = -1 on them), every fine tooth and drive fine tooth mirrored once per reversal of its gear. A candidate correction found here (4.3), not built. It makes T S T = S^-1 exact on the courses tried. It removes the A1 courses' clicks after the first top, including the head's bang's 64 fittings, and moves the structured worlds from tick 2 or 3.

**The rows** (Animate's to bench):
- **Row 1** (the bangs recur): yes on every course here.
- **Row 1c:** RL's bangs are copies. With the head's turnover V's differ while fittings come, and at 5,000,000 the rises repeat in period two after the third bang. With the flows odd under T every A1 bang is a copy.
- **Row 46:** not shown on any course measured.

**Contradictions with nature it leaves:**
- The swing's top in V is the lattice's last Link rate, where p_0 = 22, not 0 (the physicist's artifact).
- The flat torus still has no brake (draft C's C1).
- R's swing is set by the matter's spring and the held field.

## 6. Sofer's rules, self-checked

- **Numbers:** none beyond 0 and 1 and the counts d + 1 and d - 1. 2 is the Spoke's two sides. Gamma is the calibre's.
- **No float, root, cosine or table** in the formulas. The probes are integer only, beyond the timing prints.
- **Flagged for Sofer:**
  - n = (p_m + Gamma - 1) div Gamma is an indicator spelt as Rule3's quotient: a coefficient, not a cap on a state integer. It is BH-S1's own mask as a product.
  - X_f = Gamma is a comparison with a value: the step's edge, no typed number, but a bound.
- **The mutation proof:** every probe carries its mutants and their failing outputs (sections 2.4, 3.2 and 4.3). One mutant is not caught (the fine tooth's reset in p3), and it is named.
- **Quotation:** lines 132, 1608 and 1909 of docs/ALGEBRA.md at scribe-5 f32b67cc3 (section 1).

## 7. OPEN

1. **Sofer:** n under "no bound and no floor" and law 11; X_f = Gamma under "no floor value".
2. **The asynchronous dial** (n with its remainder kept): the step's coefficients without the rate they carry now, and law 4 read at the clock's own ticks.
3. **The wall without a mask:** the grip read from both ends' rates. It moves every non-uniform board; not run.
4. **The click that makes every bang show more** (rows 46 and 1c) with the rewind exact. The click must break T S T = S^-1 and not die out.
5. **The flows odd under T** (4.3): a change of law 4's mirror, Sofer's and David's. Checked on V, RL, the head's bang, the hole and nature only. The offset's three odd gears and the twin hands' lifts were not read for their sign under T.
6. **T S T = S^-1 at any teeth count** (the mathematician's): here only at the probes' teeth.
7. **RL on the structured charts:** its grips act on L instead of X; not run.
8. **The 5^3 bang:** not run, since the wave setting needs a side divisible by 4. The 5^3 runs are the sea's.

**Runs:** each under 3 minutes, except the hole at 900 ticks (4 minutes 15 seconds, three side by side).

== t24h.py ==
"""The triggers' harness: the head's engine (the repo's src at b56643b0e = engine-5.0-test's src, 834cfd72e) with run-time
variants of four of its acts, written apart from drafts B, C and their verifiers (no import from them). No file of the
repository is edited: every variant is a function assigned on the head's classes at run time, and `head()` puts the
head's own functions back.

The switches (V):
  drive   'head'  the head's typed held numerator with F1's clamp (E10, E11)
          'V'     draft C's derived push, GR's volume equation: X ((d+1) G + 2d V) over Gamma key
          'R'     GR's 00 equation in the stretch X: X'' = X'^2 / (2X) - X ((d+1) K - (d-1) V) / (Gamma key)
          'RL'    the same 00 equation in the Link's length L (X = L L div Gamma): L'' = -L ((d+1) K - (d-1) V) / (2 Gamma key)
  vel     for 'R': 'before' the velocity term read from the gear's before slot in both goings (draft C's verifier's form),
          'going' read from the other slot in the going (forward X-, backward X+)
  spring  the regulator's own spring D_R (True on the head; False in draft C's work/)
  stop    'restore' BH-S1's restore of every gear at a stopped clock (the head)
          'a'       the rate 0 gives nothing on the time gear: n = (p_m + Gamma - 1) div Gamma, X_out = X_held + n (X_step - X_held),
                    no restore of any other gear
          'a_all'   the same n on every gear (the restore spelt as the product)
          'free'    nothing held (draft C's vol0_free)
  mask    the stopped mask on the Meshes' factors (True on the head)
  bottom  'floor19' law 4's typed floor X_f = stretch_of(Gamma, -Gamma) (the head)
          'floorG'  X_f = Gamma, the full rate
          'wmin'    the time gear's weak minimum X0 >= X1 <= X2, one strict, and no X_f
          'none'    no bottom trigger
  low     the load reading's deepest hill, in Gammas (-1 on the head: stretch.LOW)
Integers only; run with /home/user/Universe24/venv314/bin/python -I."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import sys
from collections import Counter

sys.path[:0] = ["/home/user/Universe24/src", "/home/user/Universe24"]

import numpy as np  # noqa: E402

from event_universe import clock as CL  # noqa: E402
from event_universe import readings  # noqa: E402
from event_universe import tick as T  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.core import rates  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.core.acts import carry, mid  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.core.rule3 import Gearing, carries, held_numerator  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.core.spokes import AXES, SPOKES  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.features import stretch  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.regulation import tensor_parts  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.trains import REGULATOR, Gear  # noqa: E402
from tests.laws import node_of, run_back_exact, state_of, world_of  # noqa: E402

V: dict = {}
DEFAULT = dict(drive="head", vel="before", spring=True, stop="restore", mask=True, bottom="floor19", low=-1, uround="plus1", tflows="even")
HEAD = {
    "impulse": T.Tick.impulse,
    "carry": T.Tick.carry,
    "rate_readings": T.Tick.rate_readings,
    "turnover": CL.Clock.turnover,
    "mean_teeth": readings.mean_teeth,
    "load": readings.load,
    "LOW": stretch.LOW,
}
STATS: list = []  # one dict per forward tick


def ints(a):
    return [int(v) for v in np.asarray(a, dtype=object).reshape(-1)]


def x_of(clock, notch):
    """The stretch the engine reads from the time gear's notch: X itself, or under 'RL' X = L L div Gamma."""
    if V.get("drive") == "RL":
        return carries(notch * notch, clock.full_rate)
    return notch


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the L reading (drive 'RL')
def mean_teeth(clock, gear):
    if V.get("drive") == "RL":
        return carries(clock.teeth, 2) * (x_of(clock, gear.before) + x_of(clock, gear.now))
    return HEAD["mean_teeth"](clock, gear)


def load(clk):
    return np.asarray(stretch.load_of(clk.full_rate, x_of(clk, clk.train(REGULATOR).gears[0].now)), dtype=object)


def rate_readings(self):
    if V.get("drive") != "RL":
        return HEAD["rate_readings"](self)
    # the head's rate_readings with the stretch read as X = L L div Gamma (lines copied from tick.py:94-118)
    clock = self.clock
    self.load = np.asarray(stretch.load_of(clock.full_rate, x_of(clock, self.at(self.regulator.gears[0]))), dtype=object)
    clock.start_load = self.load
    self.rate, self.mesh_rate = readings.rates_of(clock, self.load)
    clock.start_rate = self.rate
    if self.stopped is None:
        self.stopped = readings.stopped_of(clock, self.load)
        self.still = self.top | self.stopped
    self.held_load = self.load + sum((r.weight * self.at(r.gears[0]) for r in self.fields), start=0)
    clock.start_held = self.held_load
    clock.dial = clock.dial + self.going * np.where(self.stopped, 0, self.rate)
    self.factors = T.factors(clock, [self.at(g) for g in self.regulator.gears[1 : 1 + AXES]], self.stopped)
    clock.start_factors = self.factors
    self.pawls = T.pawls(self.ratchet.gears[0].fine)
    clock.start_pawls = self.pawls
    self.clocked_offset = rates.rounded(self.at(self.offset.gears[0]) * self.rate, clock.full_rate)
    self.stretch_now = x_of(clock, self.at(self.regulator.gears[0]))
    clock.start_stretch = self.stretch_now
    self.flow_gears = [self.at(g) for g in self.regulator.gears[1 + AXES : 1 + AXES + AXES]]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the impulse: the forms split (K, G, V)
def impulse_even(self):
    """tick.Tick.impulse (tick.py:203-244) line for line with the strides' odd rounding read at 2 P in place of 2 P + 1
    (switch uround='even'): the rounding odd about P = 0, the one mirror's fixed point, in place of P = -1/2, the old flip's."""
    from event_universe import hands as HN
    from event_universe.meshes import coupling_of, cross
    from event_universe.regulation import stress_of
    clock = self.clock
    self.forms = np.zeros(clock.size, dtype=object)
    self.impelled = {}
    d = (clock.teeth + clock.teeth) * self.stretch_now
    w_side = readings.mean_teeth(self.clock, self.regulator.gears[0])
    sweep = np.zeros(clock.size, dtype=object)
    self.stress = [np.zeros(clock.size, dtype=object) for _ in range(REGULATOR - 1)]
    self.masses = np.zeros(clock.size, dtype=object)
    for r in self.driven:
        k = self.where[id(r)]
        p_side = [w_side * (gear.now - gear.before) + gear.fine - mid(w_side) for gear in self.trains[k]]
        p_other = [p + self.going * self.stretch_now * f for p, f in zip(p_side, self.pull[k], strict=True)]
        if r is self.offset:
            clock.start_kicks = []
            for a, (gear, n) in enumerate(zip(self.trains[k], [sweep, *self.lead], strict=True)):
                q, gear.dfine = carry(n, gear.dteeth, gear.dfine, self.going)
                p_other[a] = p_other[a] + self.going * clock.teeth * self.stretch_now * q
                clock.start_kicks.append(q)
        if r.weight is not None:
            gear = self.trains[k][0]
            q, gear.dfine = carry(r.weight * self.masses, gear.dteeth, gear.dfine, self.going)
            p_other[0] = p_other[0] + self.going * clock.teeth * self.stretch_now * q
        u_in = [rates.rounded(p + p, d) for p in p_side]
        u_out = [rates.rounded(p + p, d) for p in p_other]
        if r in self.hands:
            velocity = [x + y for x, y in zip(u_in, u_out, strict=True)]
            sweep = sweep + coupling_of(r) * cross(*velocity, *self.starts[k])
            found = stress_of(self.starts[k], velocity)
            self.stress = [s_ + r.ratio[0] * t for s_, t in zip(self.stress, found, strict=True)]
        mass = self.train_spring[k] * HN.dot(self.starts[k], self.starts[k])
        self.masses = self.masses + carries(mass, self.train_teeth[k])
        pulls = HN.products(self.starts[k], self.pull[k])
        for product, pull in zip(HN.products(u_in, u_out), pulls, strict=True):
            self.forms = self.forms + product + carries(-pull, self.train_teeth[k])
        self.impelled[k] = p_other


def impulse(self):
    if V.get("uround") == "even":
        impulse_even(self)
    else:
        HEAD["impulse"](self)
    plus = 0 if V.get("uround") == "even" else 1
    clock = self.clock
    d = (clock.teeth + clock.teeth) * self.stretch_now
    w_side = readings.mean_teeth(clock, self.regulator.gears[0])
    self.v_pot = np.zeros(clock.size, dtype=object)
    self.v_spr = np.zeros(clock.size, dtype=object)
    self.v_kin = np.zeros(clock.size, dtype=object)
    for r in self.driven:
        k = self.where[id(r)]
        p_side = [w_side * (g.now - g.before) + g.fine - mid(w_side) for g in self.trains[k]]
        u_in = [rates.rounded(p + p + plus, d) for p in p_side]
        u_out = [rates.rounded(p + p + plus, d) for p in self.impelled[k]]
        for a, f, ui, uo in zip(self.starts[k], self.pull[k], u_in, u_out, strict=True):
            self.v_pot = self.v_pot - a * f
            self.v_spr = self.v_spr + self.train_spring[k] * a * a
            self.v_kin = self.v_kin + ui * uo


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the carry with the switches
def gate(clock, mesh_rate):
    """(a): the clock's own tick at its Link rate, n = (p_m + Gamma - 1) div Gamma: 1 at every Link rate from 1 to Gamma, 0 at 0."""
    return carries(np.asarray(mesh_rate, dtype=object) + clock.full_rate - 1, clock.full_rate)


def held_by(n, held, out):
    """Every integer of a gear as held + n (out - held): out where n = 1, held where n = 0."""
    g = Gear(held.now + n * (out.now - held.now), held.before + n * (out.before - held.before),
             held.fine + n * (out.fine - held.fine), out.teeth, None if out.dfine is None else held.dfine + n * (out.dfine - held.dfine),
             out.dteeth)
    if np.ndim(out.teeth) and np.ndim(held.teeth):
        g.teeth = held.teeth + n * (out.teeth - held.teeth)
    return g


def time_numerator(self, k):
    clk = self.clock
    X = self.stretch_now
    gear = self.trains[k][0]
    scale = clk.trace * clk.rim * clk.full_rate * clk.full_rate * clk.full_rate  # the drive teeth over Gamma key
    mode = V["drive"]
    if mode == "head":
        p02, (trace, _t) = self.rate * self.rate, tensor_parts(self.stress[:AXES])
        drive = held_numerator(clk.trace, X, self.forms, trace, self.rate, clk.rim, clk.full_rate)
        hold = X * gear.dteeth
        return np.minimum(np.maximum(drive, -hold), hold)
    g_wind = carries(self.v_pot - self.v_spr, clk.teeth)
    v_wind = carries(self.v_spr, clk.teeth)
    if mode == "V":
        return X * ((AXES + 1) * g_wind + SPOKES * v_wind) * scale
    if mode == "R":
        other = gear.before if (V["vel"] == "before" or self.going == 1) else gear.now
        vel = carries(clk.full_rate * clk.key * (X - other) * (X - other), X + X)
        return (vel - X * ((AXES + 1) * self.v_kin - (AXES - 1) * v_wind)) * scale
    if mode == "RL":
        L = self.at(gear)
        assert scale % 2 == 0
        return -L * ((AXES + 1) * self.v_kin - (AXES - 1) * v_wind) * carries(scale, 2)
    raise ValueError(mode)


def carry_variant(self):
    """tick.Tick.carry (tick.py:246-287) line for line, with the drive, the regulator's spring and the stopped clock's hold
    switched; every other line the head's."""
    clk, regulator = self.clock, self.regulator
    k = self.where[id(regulator)]
    p02, (trace, traceless) = self.rate * self.rate, tensor_parts(self.stress[:AXES])
    numerators = [time_numerator(self, k)]
    numerators += [part * p02 for part in (*traceless, *self.stress[AXES:])]
    gearing = readings.gearing_of(clk, regulator, self.load, self.factors)
    if not V["spring"]:
        gearing = Gearing(gearing.grips, gearing.teeth + gearing.teeth - sum(gearing.grips), gearing.teeth, 0)
    out = clk.act(self.trains[k], gearing, None, numerators, self.going, None, *self.ends(regulator))[-1]
    self.time_step = out[0].copy()  # the time gear's own step before any hold (the head discards it at a stopped clock)
    n = None
    if V["stop"] in ("a", "a_p0", "a_plus1"):
        n = gate(clk, self.rate if V["stop"] == "a_p0" else self.mesh_rate)  # mutant a_p0: the clock's rate in place of the Link rate
        held_time = self.held[k][0]
        held_gear = held_by(n, held_time, out[0])
        if V["stop"] == "a_plus1":  # mutant: the held time gear left one notch up
            held_gear.now = held_gear.now + (1 - n)
        out = [held_gear, *out[1:]]
    self.out[k] = out
    w_div = self.end_teeth(self.out[k][0])
    for r in self.driven:
        kk = self.where[id(r)]
        new = []
        for a, p, gear in zip(self.starts[kk], self.impelled[kk], self.trains[kk], strict=True):
            v, fine = carry(p, w_div, mid(w_div))
            now, before = (a + self.going * v, a)[:: self.going]
            new.append(Gear(now, before, fine, w_div, gear.dfine, gear.dteeth))
        if self.going == -1 and self.kept is not None:
            clk.restore(new, self.kept[kk], self.top)
        self.out[kk] = new
        if r in self.hands:
            start = self.trains[kk] if self.going == 1 else new
            after = new if self.going == 1 else self.held[kk]
            self.hand_starts.append(list(start))
            self.before_turnover.append([gear.copy() for gear in after])
    clk.before_turnover = self.before_turnover
    clk.set_trains(self.out)
    if self.going == 1:
        self.advances()
    if self.going == -1 and self.kept is not None:
        clk.restore(clk.all_gears(), [g for train in self.kept for g in train], self.stopped & self.top)
    if V["stop"] == "restore":
        clk.restore(clk.all_gears(), [g for train in self.held for g in train], self.stopped & ~self.top)
    if V["stop"] == "restore_but_time":  # mutant: BH-S1's restore of every gear but the time gear
        gears = clk.all_gears()
        helds = [g for train in self.held for g in train]
        tg = self.trains[k][0] if False else clk.train(REGULATOR).gears[0]
        keep = [(g, h) for g, h in zip(gears, helds, strict=True) if g is not tg]
        clk.restore([g for g, _ in keep], [h for _, h in keep], self.stopped & ~self.top)
    if V["stop"] == "a_all":  # every gear of every train held by the same n, after the advances, where the head restores
        n = gate(clk, self.mesh_rate) + np.asarray(self.top, dtype=object)  # a turned clock is the turnover's, as the head's mask ~top
        n = n - carries(n, 2) * (n - 1)  # n = 1 where the clock is live or turned, 0 where stopped and not turned
        for g, h in zip(clk.all_gears(), [g for train in self.held for g in train], strict=True):
            hb = held_by(n, h, g)
            g.now, g.before, g.fine = hb.now, hb.before, hb.fine
            if g.dfine is not None:
                g.dfine = hb.dfine
            if np.ndim(g.teeth) and np.ndim(h.teeth):
                g.teeth = hb.teeth


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the turnover with the bottom switched
def turnover_variant(self, held, going=1, top=None):
    if going == -1:
        if top.any():
            self.reverse(top, held)
        return top
    k = self.trains_.index(self.train(REGULATOR))
    x0, x1, x2 = (x_of(self, v) for v in (held[k][0].before, held[k][0].now, self.train(REGULATOR).gears[0].now))
    peak = (x0 <= x1) & (x2 <= x1) & ((x0 < x1) | (x2 < x1))
    b = V["bottom"]
    if b == "floor19":
        xf = readings.bottom(self)
        low = (x0 < xf) | (x2 < xf)
    elif b == "floorG":
        low = (x0 < self.full_rate) | (x2 < self.full_rate)
    elif b == "wmin":
        low = (x0 >= x1) & (x2 >= x1) & ((x0 > x1) | (x2 > x1))
    elif b == "smin":  # the strict minimum X0 > X1 < X2 (not the top's mirror: both strict), for comparison
        low = (x0 > x1) & (x2 > x1)
    elif b == "none":
        low = np.zeros(self.size, dtype=bool)
    else:
        raise ValueError(b)
    top = peak | low
    self.turned_over = top
    self._peak, self._low = peak, low & ~peak
    if top.any():
        self.counts["turns"] += int(top.sum())
        self.turnovers.append((self.ticks, int(top.sum())))
        self.reverse(top, held)
    return top


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the turnover's reverse with the flows odd (switch tflows)
def reverse_variant(self, top, held):
    """Clock.reverse (clock.py:228-238) with the regulator's flow gears odd under T when V['tflows'] == 'odd': their two
    notches exchanged and negated (K = -1 on them, as on a hand's twelve gear), every fine tooth and drive fine tooth
    mirrored once per reversal of its gear (twice on a negated gear: kept). 'even' is the head's."""
    from event_universe import hands as HN
    from event_universe.core import acts as A
    odd = V.get("tflows") == "odd"
    for r, train, held_train in zip(self.trains_, self.trains(), held, strict=True):
        signs = list(HN.signs(r.size))
        if odd and r.size == REGULATOR:
            signs = [1] * (1 + AXES) + [-1] * AXES
        nows, befores, fines = A.reverse(held_train, signs)
        times = [1 + (sg < 0) for sg in signs] if odd else [1] * len(signs)
        drives = [None if h.dfine is None else A.mirror(h.dfine, h.dteeth, t) for h, t in zip(held_train, times)]
        fines = fines if r.teeth is None else [A.flip(h.fine, h.teeth, 1) for h in held_train]
        reversed_gears = [Gear(n, b, f, h.teeth, d, h.dteeth) for n, b, f, h, d in zip(nows, befores, fines, held_train, drives, strict=True)]
        self.restore(train, reversed_gears, top)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the tick's statistics (forward)
def tick_turnover(self):
    self.top = self.clock.turnover(self.held)
    if self.going != 1:
        return
    c = self.clock
    k = self.where[id(self.regulator)]
    stopped = np.asarray(self.stopped, dtype=bool)
    top = np.asarray(self.top, dtype=bool)
    x1 = x_of(c, self.held[k][0].now)
    x0 = x_of(c, self.held[k][0].before)
    step = x_of(c, self.time_step.now)
    coast = [int(v) for v in np.asarray(step - x1, dtype=object)[stopped]] if stopped.any() else []
    xs = ints(x_of(c, c.train(REGULATOR).gears[0].now))
    STATS.append({
        "tick": c.ticks, "x_min": min(xs), "x_max": max(xs), "x_start_min": min(ints(x1)), "x_start_max": max(ints(x1)),
        "turned": int(top.sum()), "peak": int(np.asarray(getattr(c, "_peak", top), dtype=bool).sum()) if hasattr(c, "_peak") else None,
        "low": int(np.asarray(getattr(c, "_low", top & False), dtype=bool).sum()) if hasattr(c, "_low") else None,
        "stopped": int(stopped.sum()), "stopped_top": int((stopped & top).sum()), "stopped_not_top": int((stopped & ~top).sum()),
        "stopped_rising": int((stopped & (np.asarray(x0 < x1, dtype=bool))).sum()),
        "coast": coast[:1], "size": int(top.size),
        "below_gamma": int(np.asarray((x_of(c, c.train(REGULATOR).gears[0].now) < c.full_rate) | (x_of(c, c.train(REGULATOR).gears[0].before) < c.full_rate), dtype=bool).sum()),
        "births": c.counts["births"], "turns": c.counts["turns"],
    })


def install(**changes):
    V.clear()
    V.update(DEFAULT)
    V.update(changes)
    STATS.clear()
    T.Tick.impulse = impulse
    T.Tick.carry = carry_variant
    T.Tick.rate_readings = rate_readings
    T.Tick.turnover = tick_turnover
    CL.Clock.turnover = turnover_variant
    CL.Clock.reverse = reverse_variant if V.get("tflows") == "odd" else HEAD_REVERSE
    readings.mean_teeth = mean_teeth
    readings.load = load
    stretch.LOW = V["low"]
    if not V["mask"]:
        T.factors = lambda clock, axis_notches=None, stopped=None, _f=HEAD_FACTORS: _f(clock, axis_notches, None)
    else:
        T.factors = HEAD_FACTORS


HEAD_FACTORS = T.factors


def head():
    """The head's own functions back."""
    T.Tick.impulse, T.Tick.carry, T.Tick.rate_readings = HEAD["impulse"], HEAD["carry"], HEAD["rate_readings"]
    T.Tick.turnover = HEAD_TICK_TURNOVER
    CL.Clock.turnover = HEAD["turnover"]
    CL.Clock.reverse = HEAD_REVERSE
    readings.mean_teeth, readings.load = HEAD["mean_teeth"], HEAD["load"]
    stretch.LOW = HEAD["LOW"]
    T.factors = HEAD_FACTORS
    V.clear()


HEAD_TICK_TURNOVER = T.Tick.turnover
HEAD_REVERSE = CL.Clock.reverse


def hash_of(c):
    return hashlib.sha256(state_of(c).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]


def kinds_of(entries):
    """The click kinds of log entries: fitting (birth), unspringing (birth with exact_lay), chime (credit with a take),
    still chime (credit laid back), split; the jams by cause apart; books apart."""
    out = Counter()
    for e in entries:
        ev = e["event"]
        if ev == "birth":
            out["unspringing" if "exact_lay" in e else "fitting"] += 1
        elif ev == "credit":
            out["still chime" if "laid_back" in e else "chime"] += 1
        elif ev == "split":
            out["split"] += 1
        elif ev == "refused":
            out["jam:" + str(e.get("cause"))] += 1
        elif ev == "books":
            out["books"] += 1
    return out


def clock_of(world, calibre_rows=None, seed=None, shape=None):
    calibre, piece = world_of(world)
    import json
    calibre, piece = json.loads(json.dumps(calibre)), json.loads(json.dumps(piece))
    if calibre_rows is not None:
        calibre["rows"] = calibre_rows
    if seed is not None:
        piece["seed"] = seed
    if shape is not None:
        piece["shape"] = shape
    return CL.Clock(calibre, piece)

== course.py ==
"""The course of a board under the harness's switches: the stretch per tick, the whole-board and local turnovers (and
which trigger fired), the stopped clocks and whether each turned, the time gear's discarded step at a stopped clock,
the clicks by kind per swing, the state hash per tick (for lockstep comparisons) and the rewind through a window.

Usage: python -I course.py NAME WORLD TICKS key=value ...
  keys: the harness's switches (drive, vel, spring, stop, mask, bottom, low), and
        seed=bang|sea|sea+light|plain   (bang: the world's own seed; sea: row 2 at 'quanta' sense 1;
                                          sea+light: the sea and the world's light wave at 'light' quanta)
        quanta=Q light=L shape=S rewind=FROM:N every=E
Writes nothing but stdout (tee it)."""

import json
import sys
import time

sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/triggers")
import t24h as H  # noqa: E402

name, world, ticks = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
opts = dict(kv.split("=", 1) for kv in sys.argv[4:])
switches = {k: (v if k not in ("spring", "mask") else v == "1") for k, v in opts.items() if k in H.DEFAULT}
if "low" in switches:
    switches["low"] = int(switches["low"])
seed_kind = opts.get("seed", "bang")
calibre, piece = H.world_of(world)
seed = None
if seed_kind == "sea":
    seed = [{"lay": "sea", "row": int(opts.get("row", 2)), "quanta": int(opts.get("quanta", 20)), "sense": int(opts.get("sense", 1))}]
elif seed_kind == "sea+light":
    light = dict(piece["seed"][0], quanta=int(opts.get("light", 500)))
    seed = [{"lay": "sea", "row": int(opts.get("row", 2)), "quanta": int(opts.get("quanta", 20)), "sense": int(opts.get("sense", 1))}, light]
elif seed_kind == "bang" and "light" in opts:
    seed = [dict(piece["seed"][0], quanta=int(opts["light"]))]
shape = None if "shape" not in opts else [int(opts["shape"])] * 3

H.install(**switches)
t0 = time.time()
c = H.clock_of(world, seed=seed, shape=shape)
every = int(opts.get("every", 10))
hashes, xs, segs = [H.hash_of(c)], [], []
whole, local, log_at = [], 0, [len(c.log)]
err = None
try:
    for t in range(1, ticks + 1):
        c.tick()
        hashes.append(H.hash_of(c))
        log_at.append(len(c.log))
        s = H.STATS[-1]
        xs.append((s["x_min"], s["x_max"]))
        if s["turned"] == s["size"]:
            whole.append(t)
        else:
            local += s["turned"]
except Exception as e:  # a refusal of the engine is a finding: kept and printed
    err = f"{type(e).__name__} at tick {c.ticks}: {e}"
done = len(xs)
st = H.STATS[:done]
print(f"{name}: world {world} shape {c.size} seed {seed_kind} {json.dumps({k: opts[k] for k in opts if k in ('quanta', 'light', 'row', 'sense')})} switches {json.dumps(H.V)}")
print(f"  ran {done} of {ticks} ticks in {time.time() - t0:.0f} s" + (f"; STOPPED: {err}" if err else ""))
print("  X: " + "  ".join(f"t{t}:{v[0]}" + ("" if v[0] == v[1] else f"..{v[1]}") for t, v in enumerate(xs, 1) if t % every == 0 or t in whole))
print(f"  whole-board turnovers {len(whole)} at {whole[:60]}{' ...' if len(whole) > 60 else ''}; local turnovers {local}")
peaks = sum(s["peak"] or 0 for s in st)
lows = sum(s["low"] or 0 for s in st)
print(f"  turned clock-ticks by the top (weak maximum) {peaks}, by the bottom trigger alone {lows}")
print(f"  least X at a tick's end {min(v[0] for v in xs) if xs else None}; clock-ticks ending with a time-gear slot below Gamma {sum(s['below_gamma'] for s in st)}")
stopped = sum(s["stopped"] for s in st)
print(f"  stopped clock-ticks {stopped}: turned at that tick {sum(s['stopped_top'] for s in st)}, not turned {sum(s['stopped_not_top'] for s in st)}, rising into the stop {sum(s['stopped_rising'] for s in st)}")
coasts = [(s["tick"], s["coast"][0]) for s in st if s["coast"]]
if coasts:
    print(f"  the time gear's own step at a stopped clock (X_step - X_start, the head discards it), first per stopped tick: {coasts[:12]}")
# the clicks per swing: the segments between whole-board turnovers
bounds = [0, *whole, done]
print(f"  births {c.counts['births']}; clicks per swing (ticks a..b: kinds):")
rows = []
for a, b in zip(bounds, bounds[1:]):
    if b <= a:
        continue
    k = H.kinds_of(c.log[log_at[a]: log_at[b]])
    clicks = {kk: v for kk, v in sorted(k.items()) if not kk.startswith("jam:") and kk != "books"}
    jams = sum(v for kk, v in k.items() if kk.startswith("jam:"))
    xm = min(v[0] for v in xs[a:b])
    xM = max(v[1] for v in xs[a:b])
    rows.append((a + 1, b, clicks, jams, xm, xM))
    print(f"    {a + 1}..{b}: X {xm}..{xM}; clicks {clicks}; jams {jams}")
# a swing that repeats the one before: the X course per tick alike
rep = []
for i in range(2, len(bounds) - 1):
    a0, b0, a1, b1 = bounds[i - 2], bounds[i - 1], bounds[i - 1], bounds[i]
    if b0 - a0 == b1 - a1 and xs[a0:b0] == xs[a1:b1][::-1]:
        rep.append((a1 + 1, b1, "the course of the swing before, reversed"))
    elif b0 - a0 == b1 - a1 and xs[a0:b0] == xs[a1:b1]:
        rep.append((a1 + 1, b1, "the course of the swing before"))
print(f"  swings whose X course repeats the one before: {rep if rep else 'none'}")
dup = {}
for t, h in enumerate(hashes):
    dup.setdefault(h, []).append(t)
same = [v for v in dup.values() if len(v) > 1]
print(f"  states met twice or more (exact recurrence): {same[:6] if same else 'none'}")
print(f"  final state hash {hashes[-1]}")
if "xs" in opts:
    with open(opts["xs"], "w") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(f"{t} {v[0]} {v[1]}" for t, v in enumerate(xs, 1)) + "\n")
# every pair of swings (not only neighbours) whose X course is the same, as is or reversed
pairs = []
segs2 = [(a, b) for a, b in zip(bounds, bounds[1:]) if b > a]
for i in range(1, len(segs2)):
    for j in range(i + 1, len(segs2)):
        (a0, b0), (a1, b1) = segs2[i], segs2[j]
        if b0 - a0 == b1 - a1 and (xs[a0:b0] == xs[a1:b1] or xs[a0:b0] == xs[a1:b1][::-1]):
            pairs.append((f"{a0 + 1}..{b0}", f"{a1 + 1}..{b1}"))
print(f"  pairs of whole swings with the same X course (as is or reversed): {pairs[:12] if pairs else 'none'}{' ...' if len(pairs) > 12 else ''} ({len(pairs)})")
if "hashes" in opts:
    with open(opts["hashes"], "w") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(hashes) + "\n")
if "rewind" in opts and not err:
    frm, n = (int(v) for v in opts["rewind"].split(":"))
    H.install(**switches)
    c2 = H.clock_of(world, seed=seed, shape=shape)
    for _ in range(frm):
        c2.tick()
    got = H.run_back_exact(c2, n)
    turned_in = [t for t in whole if frm < t <= frm + n]
    print(f"  rewind from tick {frm}: {got} of {n} states returned exactly (whole-board turnovers inside the window: {turned_in})")
H.head()

== p1_rate0.py ==
"""p1, (a) "the rate 0 gives nothing": at a clock whose Link rate p_m reads 0 at the tick's start, the time gear's step is
weighted by the Link rate's own tick count n = (p_m + Gamma - 1) div Gamma (1 at every p_m from 1 to Gamma, 0 at 0), and
no gear is restored (BH-S1's restore struck, its hold on the time gear alone kept as the product); law 4's own weak
maximum then turns the clock. Claims checked here, per case:
  C1  every stopped clock-tick is rising into the stop (X0 < X1) and turned at that tick (stopped & ~top = 0);
  C2  (a) is the head bit for bit (BH-S1's restore of every gear, FR-S1's trigger on the restored gear);
  C3  the restore spelt as the product on every gear ('a_all') is the head bit for bit;
  C4  the head's discarded step at a stopped clock is the time gear's coasting, X_step - X1 > 0.
Mutants, each must differ from the head (caught):
  a_p0              n read from the clock's rate p_0 in place of the Link rate (p_0 = 22 at the stop, so n = 1)
  a_plus1           the held time gear left one notch up (X2 = X1 + 1, no weak maximum)
  restore_but_time  BH-S1's restore of every gear but the time gear
  free              nothing held (draft C's vol0_free)
Usage: python -I p1_rate0.py CASE VARIANT,...   CASE: V5M (the bang at 5,000,000 quanta, draft C's derived push, 250 ticks),
       V500k (500,000 quanta, 300 ticks), hole (the head's own drive on the hole, 560 ticks)."""

import sys
import time

sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/triggers")
import t24h as H  # noqa: E402

CASES = {
    "V5M": ("world_bang.json", dict(drive="V", spring=False), None, 250),
    "V500k": ("world_bang.json", dict(drive="V", spring=False), 500000, 300),
    "hole": ("world_hole.json", {}, None, 560),
}
case, variants = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2].split(",")
world, base, light, ticks = CASES[case]


def run(stop):
    H.install(stop=stop, **base)
    seed = None
    if light is not None:
        seed = [dict(H.world_of(world)[1]["seed"][0], quanta=light)]
    c = H.clock_of(world, seed=seed)
    hashes = [H.hash_of(c)]
    for _ in range(ticks):
        c.tick()
        hashes.append(H.hash_of(c))
    st = list(H.STATS)
    H.head()
    return hashes, st, c


ref = None
cases = fails = 0
for v in ["restore", *[x for x in variants if x != "restore"]]:
    t0 = time.time()
    hashes, st, c = run(v)
    if ref is None:
        ref = hashes
    diff = [t for t in range(len(ref)) if ref[t] != hashes[t]]
    stopped = sum(s["stopped"] for s in st)
    s_top = sum(s["stopped_top"] for s in st)
    s_not = sum(s["stopped_not_top"] for s in st)
    rising = sum(s["stopped_rising"] for s in st)
    coast = [s["coast"][0] for s in st if s["coast"]]
    tops = [s["tick"] for s in st if s["turned"] == s["size"]]
    mutant = v in ("a_p0", "a_plus1", "restore_but_time", "free")
    if v == "restore":
        ok = s_not == 0 and rising == stopped and all(x > 0 for x in coast)
        claim = "C1 and C4 on the head"
    elif v in ("a", "a_all"):
        ok = not diff and s_not == 0
        claim = "C2" if v == "a" else "C3"
    else:
        ok = bool(diff)
        claim = "mutant caught"
    cases += 1
    fails += not ok

          f"{', first at tick ' + str(diff[0]) if diff else ''}; stopped clock-ticks {stopped}, rising {rising}, turned {s_top}, not turned {s_not};"
          f" whole-board tops {tops[:8]}; turns {c.counts['turns']}; births {c.counts['births']};"
          f" the time gear's discarded step at the stopped clocks: {len(coast)} ticks, least {min(coast) if coast else None} ({time.time() - t0:.0f} s)", flush=True)
