== p2_bottom.py ==
"""p2, the bottom without a typed floor, measured on the head's worlds against the typed floor X_f = stretch_of(Gamma,
-Gamma) = 19 (law 4's second trigger, ALGEBRA.md line 1608): each world run with the head's own drive and acts and
law 4's bottom switched (floor19 the head; floorG the full rate X_f = Gamma; wmin the weak minimum X0 >= X1 <= X2, one
strict, with no X_f; none no bottom trigger), in lockstep against the head: the first tick whose state differs, the
ticks that differ, the turns, the turns by the bottom alone, the births, the stopped clock-ticks, the least X, the
clock-ticks ending with a time-gear slot below Gamma, and a refusal of the engine if one comes.
Usage: python -I p2_bottom.py [bottoms=floor19,floorG,...] WORLD[:TICKS[:SEEDFORM]] ... (SEEDFORM chiral: the nature world's lays at 5 quanta)"""

import sys
import time

sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/triggers")
import t24h as H  # noqa: E402

ARGS = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if not a.startswith("bottoms=")]
BOTTOMS = next((a.split("=", 1)[1].split(",") for a in sys.argv[1:] if a.startswith("bottoms=")), ["floor19", "floorG", "wmin", "smin", "none"])


def seed_of(world, form):
    if form != "chiral":
        return None
    seed = H.world_of(world)[1]["seed"]
    return [dict(s, quanta=5) if "quanta" in s else s for s in seed]


def run(world, ticks, form, bottom, extra):
    H.install(bottom=bottom, **extra)
    c = H.clock_of(world, seed=seed_of(world, form))
    hashes, err = [H.hash_of(c)], None
    try:
        for _ in range(ticks):
            c.tick()
            hashes.append(H.hash_of(c))
    except Exception as e:
        err = f"{type(e).__name__} at tick {c.ticks}: {str(e)[:90]}"
    st = list(H.STATS)
    H.head()
    return hashes, st, c, err


cases = 0
for arg in ARGS:
    parts = arg.split(":")
    world, ticks, form = parts[0], int(parts[1]) if len(parts) > 1 else 60, parts[2] if len(parts) > 2 else ""
    ref = None
    for bottom in BOTTOMS:
        t0 = time.time()
        hashes, st, c, err = run(world, ticks, form, bottom, {})
        cases += 1
        if ref is None:
            ref = hashes
        n = min(len(ref), len(hashes))
        diff = [t for t in range(n) if ref[t] != hashes[t]]
        lows = sum(s["low"] or 0 for s in st)
        print(f"{world}{'(' + form + ')' if form else ''} {ticks} ticks, bottom {bottom}: ran {len(hashes) - 1}"
              f"{'; REFUSED ' + err if err else ''}; states differing from the head {len(diff)} of {n}"
              f"{', first at tick ' + str(diff[0]) if diff else ''}; turns {c.counts['turns']} (by the bottom alone {lows});"
              f" births {c.counts['births']}; stopped clock-ticks {sum(s['stopped'] for s in st)};"
              f" least X {min(s['x_min'] for s in st) if st else None};"
              f" clock-ticks ending with a slot below Gamma {sum(s['below_gamma'] for s in st)} ({time.time() - t0:.0f} s)", flush=True)
print(f"p2: {cases} runs")

== p3_edge.py ==
"""p3, the one formula's own edge at the full rate: the light's step at the wave number pi on every axis (the checkerboard,
a_j = -a across every Spoke) under the head's own Rule3 integers (rule3.gearing, acts.carry), at Link rates p_m below,
at and above Gamma. The step is w a+ + r' = (S - SUM_j R_j) a - w a- + r with S = 2w - D - SUM_j R_j (the head's hold),
so a+ + a- = (2 - (D + 2 SUM_j R_j) / w) a, and for the light (D = 0) at no tension (D + 2 SUM_j R_j) / w = 4 p_m^2 / Gamma^2:
cos omega = 1 - 2 (p_m / Gamma)^2, bounded for p_m < Gamma, a double root at p_m = Gamma (the vacuum: linear growth),
exponential growth for p_m > Gamma. Claims:
  E1  bounded at every p_m from Gamma - 50 to Gamma - 1: the last quarter's largest |a| within 11/10 of the first quarter's;
  E2  at p_m = Gamma the step is a+ = -2a - a- exactly, so from (a-, a) = (1000, 999) |a| = 1999 (k + 1) - 1000 after k ticks (linear);
  E3  above Gamma (p_m from Gamma + 1 to Gamma + 50) |a| grows past 10^6 times its start within N ticks;
  E4  the rates' table: p_m <= Gamma exactly at every load c >= 0 (c = 0 .. 7,600) and p_m > Gamma at every hill c = -1 .. -1,000,
      so X >= Gamma (load >= 0) is the step's stable side and every X < Gamma (a hill) its unstable one.
Mutants (each must break one claim): the grips' term with the sign flipped; the grips' factor 2 dropped; the edge read at
Gamma - 1; the light given a spring (pinion = rim - rim div Gamma). A fifth, the carry's fine tooth reset each tick, moves
no edge and is not caught (out/p3_edge_nofine.txt): the edge is the linear map's, not the rounding's."""

import sys

sys.path[:0] = ["/home/user/Universe24/src", "/home/user/Universe24"]

from event_universe.core import rates  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.core.acts import carry, mid  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.core.rule3 import gearing, mesh_unit_of  # noqa: E402

GAMMA, RIM, N = 1000, 15162856759112389, 2000
G = mesh_unit_of(GAMMA)


def run(pm, a0=1000, a1=999, mutant=None):
    pinion = RIM - RIM // GAMMA if mutant == "spring" else RIM  # mutant: the light given the regulator's own spring
    g = gearing(pinion, RIM, GAMMA, GAMMA, pm, None, G)  # the light: pinion = rim, spring 0
    w, hold, grips = g.teeth, g.hold, sum(g.grips)
    if mutant == "sign":
        hold = w + w + grips  # the grips' term with its sign flipped
    if mutant == "factor":
        grips = grips // 2  # the grips' factor 2 dropped (as if the pull read half the Meshes)
    a_before, a, r = a0, a1, mid(w)
    peak, size = max(abs(a0), abs(a1)), [abs(a1)]
    for _ in range(N):
        total = hold * a - w * a_before - grips * a  # SUM_j R_j a_j with a_j = -a
        nxt, r = carry(total, w, r)
        a_before, a = a, int(nxt)
        peak = max(peak, abs(a))
        size.append(abs(a))
        if peak > 10**30:
            break
    return peak, a, a_before, size


def claims(mutant=None):
    out, fails = [], 0
    edge = GAMMA - 1 if mutant == "edge" else GAMMA
    for pm in range(GAMMA - 50, GAMMA + 51):
        peak, a, ab, _ = run(pm, mutant=mutant)
        if pm < edge:
            _p, _a, _ab, size = run(pm, mutant=mutant)
            q = N // 4  # bounded: the last quarter's swing within a tenth of the first quarter's (the carry's rounding jitters it)
            ok = len(size) == N + 1 and 10 * max(size[3 * q:]) <= 11 * max(size[:q])
            tag = "E1"
        elif pm == edge:
            # exactly linear: from (a-, a) = (1000, 999), a+ = -2a - a-, so |a| after k steps is 1999 (k + 1) - 1000
            _p, _a, _ab, size = run(pm, mutant=mutant)
            ok = len(size) == N + 1 and all(size[k] == 1999 * (k + 1) - 1000 for k in range(N + 1))
            tag = "E2"
        else:
            ok = peak > 10**6 * 1000
            tag = "E3"
        fails += not ok
        out.append((pm, tag, ok, peak))
    return out, fails


def table_claim():
    fails = 0
    cases = 0
    for c in range(0, 7601):
        p0 = rates.rate(GAMMA, c)
        pm = rates.mesh_rate(GAMMA, c)
        cases += 1
        fails += not (pm <= GAMMA and p0 <= GAMMA)
    for c in range(-1, -1001, -1):
        pm = rates.mesh_rate(GAMMA, c)
        cases += 1
        fails += not (pm > GAMMA)
    return cases, fails


if __name__ == "__main__":
    out, fails = claims()
    peaks = {pm: peak for pm, _t, _o, peak in out}
    print(f"E1-E3 (the head's integers, {N} ticks, from a = 1000, a- = 999): {len(out)} Link rates, {fails} failures")
    for pm in (GAMMA - 50, GAMMA - 10, GAMMA - 1, GAMMA, GAMMA + 1, GAMMA + 10, GAMMA + 50):
        print(f"   p_m = {pm}: the largest |a| over the run {peaks[pm]}")
    _p, a, ab, size = run(GAMMA)
    print(f"   at p_m = Gamma the envelope after 1, 2, 3, 10, 100, {N} ticks: {[size[k] for k in (1, 2, 3, 10, 100, N)]} (linear: 1000 + 1999 n)")
    cases, tf = table_claim()
    print(f"E4 the rates' table at Gamma {GAMMA}: {cases} loads, {tf} failures (p_m <= Gamma at every c >= 0, p_m > Gamma at every hill)")
    total_fail = fails + tf
    for m in ("sign", "factor", "edge", "spring"):
        _o, mf = claims(m)
        print(f"   mutant {m}: {mf} failures of {len(_o)} (must be above 0)")
        total_fail += (mf == 0)
    print(f"p3: {len(out) + cases} cases, {fails + tf} failures; mutants caught {4 - (total_fail - fails - tf)} of 4")

== p4_tst.py ==
"""p4, T S T = S^-1 on the board: from the state x_t of a run, the turnover T applied to every clock (Clock.reverse, the
one mirror: every gear's two notches exchanged through the train's K, every fine tooth mirrored about its mid tooth, the
drive fine teeth mirrored, the ratchet's pawl stepped), then m ticks forward, then T again, against x_(t-m) of the same
run, gear by gear (every now, before, fine and drive fine tooth of every train at every clock). Where T S T = S^-1 holds the
run after a turnover retraces the run before it exactly, and a swing repeats the one before.
Usage: python -I p4_tst.py NAME WORLD T M key=value ... (course.py's keys: drive, vel, spring, stop, seed, quanta, light)"""

import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/triggers")
import numpy as np  # noqa: E402

import t24h as H  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.trains import REGULATOR as REGULATOR_SIZE  # noqa: E402

name, world, t_at, m = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3]), int(sys.argv[4])
opts = dict(kv.split("=", 1) for kv in sys.argv[5:])
sw = {k: (v if k not in ("spring", "mask") else v == "1") for k, v in opts.items() if k in H.DEFAULT}
seed = None
calibre, piece = H.world_of(world)
if opts.get("seed") == "sea":
    seed = [{"lay": "sea", "row": int(opts.get("row", 2)), "quanta": int(opts.get("quanta", 20)), "sense": 1}]
elif "light" in opts:
    seed = [dict(piece["seed"][0], quanta=int(opts["light"]))]


LABELS = []


def gears_of(c):
    out = []
    LABELS.clear()
    for ti, train in enumerate(c.trains()):
        for gi, g in enumerate(train):
            for sl, a in zip(("now", "before", "fine", "dfine"), (g.now, g.before, g.fine) + (() if g.dfine is None else (g.dfine,))):
                vals = [int(v) for v in np.asarray(a, dtype=object).reshape(-1)]
                out += vals
                LABELS.extend([(ti, gi, sl)] * len(vals))
    return out


def turn_all(c):
    if opts.get("mutant") == "nomirror":  # mutant: the two notches exchanged through K, no fine tooth mirrored
        from event_universe import hands
        for r, train in zip(c.trains_, c.trains(), strict=True):
            sg = hands.signs(r.size)
            for g, k in zip(train, sg if r.teeth is None else [1] * len(train)):
                g.now, g.before = k * g.before, k * g.now
        return
    if opts.get("flows") == "odd":  # candidate: the regulator's flow gears odd under T (K = -1 on them, as on a hand's
        # twelve gear), every fine tooth and drive fine tooth mirrored once per reversal of its gear (twice there: kept)
        from event_universe import hands
        from event_universe.core.acts import mirror
        from event_universe.core.spokes import AXES as AX
        for r, train in zip(c.trains_, c.trains(), strict=True):
            sg = list(hands.signs(r.size)) if r.teeth is None else [1] * len(train)
            if r.size == REGULATOR_SIZE:
                sg = [1] * (1 + AX) + [-1] * AX
            for g, k in zip(train, sg):
                n_, b_ = g.now.copy(), g.before.copy()
                g.now, g.before = k * b_, k * n_
                times = 1 + (k < 0)
                g.fine = mirror(g.fine, g.teeth, times) if r.teeth is None else __import__("event_universe.core.acts", fromlist=["flip"]).flip(g.fine, g.teeth, 1)
                if g.dfine is not None:
                    g.dfine = mirror(g.dfine, g.dteeth, times)
        return
    held = [[g.copy() for g in train] for train in c.trains()]
    c.reverse(np.ones(c.size, dtype=bool), held)


H.install(**sw)
c = H.clock_of(world, seed=seed)
kept = [gears_of(c)]
for _ in range(t_at):
    c.tick()
    kept.append(gears_of(c))
tops_before = [s["tick"] for s in H.STATS if s["turned"]]
turn_all(c)
for _ in range(m):
    c.tick()
turned_inside = [s["tick"] for s in H.STATS[t_at:] if s["turned"]]
turn_all(c)
back = gears_of(c)
want = kept[t_at - m]
same = sum(1 for a, b in zip(back, want) if a == b)
print(f"{name}: {world} switches {H.V}; T, {m} ticks forward, T, from tick {t_at}: {same} of {len(want)} integers equal x_{t_at - m}"
      f" ({len(want) - same} differ); turnovers inside the {m} ticks at {turned_inside[:6]}; clicks so far {len(c.log)}")
from collections import Counter
where = Counter(LABELS[i] for i, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(back, want)) if a != b)
if where:
    print("   differing integers by (train, gear, slot):", dict(sorted(where.items())))
H.head()

== p4b_flows.py ==
"""p4b: the regulator's flow gears on the threshold route (V, 5,000,000 quanta) from tick 10 to 15: their drive numerators
(the stress's flow parts times p_0^2), their drive fine teeth before and after each tick, and whether the drive fine tooth
or its carried remainder is 0 there (the one mirror's failing case at an even count, DAVID-TENSOR24-ANY-TEETH.md), at
every clock; and the same numerators read on the state reversed by T (do they negate?)."""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/triggers")
import numpy as np
import t24h as H
from event_universe.core.spokes import AXES
from event_universe.trains import REGULATOR
H.install(drive="V", spring=False, stop="a")
seen = []
orig = H.carry_variant
def watched(self):
    k = self.where[id(self.regulator)]
    p02 = self.rate * self.rate
    nums = [part * p02 for part in self.stress[AXES:]]
    before = [g.dfine.copy() for g in self.trains[k][1 + AXES:]]
    orig(self)
    after = [g.dfine.copy() for g in self.clock.train(REGULATOR).gears[1 + AXES:]]
    seen.append((self.clock.ticks, nums, before, after))
H.carry_variant = watched
import event_universe.tick as T
T.Tick.carry = watched
c = H.clock_of("world_bang.json")
for _ in range(15):
    c.tick()
dteeth = c.train(REGULATOR).gears[1 + AXES].dteeth
print("flow drive teeth", dteeth, "even" if dteeth % 2 == 0 else "odd")
for t, nums, before, after in seen[9:]:
    z_before = sum(int((np.asarray(b, dtype=object) == 0).sum()) for b in before)
    z_after = sum(int((np.asarray(a, dtype=object) == 0).sum()) for a in after)
    nz = sum(int((np.asarray(n, dtype=object) != 0).sum()) for n in nums)
    print(f"tick {t}: nonzero flow numerators {nz} of 192; drive fine teeth at 0 before {z_before}, after {z_after}; e.g. N at (0,0,0) {[int(n[0,0,0]) for n in nums]}")
H.head()

== p4c_flows_sign.py ==
"""p4c: the flow gears' drive numerators under the turnover (V, 5,000,000 quanta): the numerators N_t of the step from
x_(t-1) to x_t, against N' of the first step from T x_t (the step that should retrace it). A source odd under T gives
N' = -N_t; the turnover mirrors the drive fine tooth as for a source even under T (r to 2 mid - r), so with N' = -N_t
the retraced drive fine tooth misses by 2 N_t (no carry leaving: |N| << T_d). Also the time gear's numerator (the push)."""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/triggers")
import numpy as np
import t24h as H
import event_universe.tick as T
from event_universe.core.spokes import AXES
from event_universe.trains import REGULATOR
H.install(drive="V", spring=False, stop="a")
log = []
orig = H.carry_variant
def watched(self):
    k = self.where[id(self.regulator)]
    p02 = self.rate * self.rate
    flows = [part * p02 for part in self.stress[AXES:]]
    push = H.time_numerator(self, k)
    log.append((flows, push, [g.dfine.copy() for g in self.trains[k][1 + AXES:]]))
    orig(self)
T.Tick.carry = watched
c = H.clock_of("world_bang.json")
for _ in range(14):
    c.tick()
n_t = log[-1]
dfine_after = [g.dfine.copy() for g in c.train(REGULATOR).gears[1 + AXES:]]
held = [[g.copy() for g in train] for train in c.trains()]
c.reverse(np.ones(c.size, dtype=bool), held)
c.tick()
n_rev = log[-1]
cases = 0
odd = even = 0
for a, b in zip(n_t[0], n_rev[0]):
    for x, y in zip(np.asarray(a, dtype=object).reshape(-1), np.asarray(b, dtype=object).reshape(-1)):
        cases += 1
        odd += int(y) == -int(x)
        even += int(y) == int(x)
pe = sum(int(y) == int(x) for x, y in zip(np.asarray(n_t[1], dtype=object).reshape(-1), np.asarray(n_rev[1], dtype=object).reshape(-1)))
print(f"flow numerators: {cases} at 64 clocks x 3 flows; the retracing step's N' = -N_t at {odd}, N' = +N_t at {even}")
print(f"the time gear's push: N' = +N_t at {pe} of 64 (even under T)")
# the drive fine tooth: forward r -> r + N (no carry); the turnover mirrors it, m(r + N); the retracing step adds N' = -N
from event_universe.core.acts import mirror
g = c.train(REGULATOR).gears[1 + AXES]
miss = 0
for a in range(3):
    r0 = np.asarray(n_t[2][a], dtype=object).reshape(-1)
    r1 = np.asarray(dfine_after[a], dtype=object).reshape(-1)
    N = np.asarray(n_t[0][a], dtype=object).reshape(-1)
    for x0, x1, nn in zip(r0, r1, N):
        T_d = g.dteeth
        back = (mirror(int(x1), T_d, 1) - int(nn)) % T_d   # the retracing step's drive tooth with N' = -N
        miss += back != mirror(int(x0), T_d, 1)
print(f"the drive fine tooth after T and the retracing step, against T of the tooth before: {miss} of 192 miss (each by 2 N mod T_d)")
H.head()

== p5_period.py ==
"""p5, what returns and what does not, one swing apart: on a course whose X repeats swing after swing (RL on the sea),
the state at tick t against tick t + P (P the swing's length), part by part: every train's gears, and the clock's other
integers (the dial, the clicks, the slack, the bobs' wound, crossed, beats and reference). Usage: python -I p5_period.py
WORLD T P TICKS key=value ..."""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/triggers")
import numpy as np
import t24h as H
world, t_at, period = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3])
opts = dict(kv.split("=", 1) for kv in sys.argv[4:])
sw = {k: (v if k not in ("spring", "mask") else v == "1") for k, v in opts.items() if k in H.DEFAULT}
seed = [{"lay": "sea", "row": 2, "quanta": int(opts.get("quanta", 20)), "sense": 1}] if opts.get("seed") == "sea" else None
H.install(**sw)
c = H.clock_of(world, seed=seed)
def parts(c):
    out = {}
    for ti, train in enumerate(c.trains()):
        out[f"train {ti} gears"] = [int(v) for g in train for a in (g.now, g.before, g.fine) + (() if g.dfine is None else (g.dfine,)) for v in np.asarray(a, dtype=object).reshape(-1)]
    out["dial"] = [int(v) for v in c.dial.reshape(-1)]
    out["clicks"] = [int(v) for v in c.clicks.reshape(-1)]
    out["slack"] = [int(v) for v in c.slack.reshape(-1)]
    out["bobs (wound, crossed, beats, reference)"] = [int(v) for arrs in (c.wound, c.crossed, c.beats) for a in arrs for v in a.reshape(-1)] + [int(v) for pr in c.reference for a in pr for v in a.reshape(-1)]
    return out
snaps = {}
for t in range(1, t_at + 2 * period + 1):
    c.tick()
    if t in (t_at, t_at + period, t_at + 2 * period):
        snaps[t] = parts(c)
for k in snaps[t_at]:
    a, b, b2 = snaps[t_at][k], snaps[t_at + period][k], snaps[t_at + 2 * period][k]
    print(f"  {k}: tick {t_at} vs {t_at + period}: {sum(x != y for x, y in zip(a, b))} of {len(a)} differ; vs {t_at + 2 * period}: {sum(x != y for x, y in zip(a, b2))}")
H.head()

== cmp_hashes.py ==
"""The first tick at which two runs' states differ, from their per-tick hash files (course.py hashes=...)."""
import sys
a = open(sys.argv[1]).read().split(); b = open(sys.argv[2]).read().split()
n = min(len(a), len(b))
diff = [t for t in range(n) if a[t] != b[t]]
print(f"{sys.argv[1]} vs {sys.argv[2]}: {n} states compared (ticks 0..{n-1}), {len(diff)} differ" + (f", the first at tick {diff[0]}" if diff else ": bit for bit"))

== trace_drive.py ==
"""A trace of the time gear's drive at the clock (0, 0, 0): X, its before, K, V (over T), the velocity term and the
drive's quotient, per forward tick. Usage: python -I trace_drive.py WORLD TICKS key=value ... (course.py's keys)"""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/triggers")
import t24h as H
from event_universe import tick as T
from event_universe.core.rule3 import carries
world, ticks = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2])
opts = dict(kv.split("=", 1) for kv in sys.argv[3:])
sw = {k: (v if k not in ("spring", "mask") else v == "1") for k, v in opts.items() if k in H.DEFAULT}
calibre, piece = H.world_of(world)
rows = None
if opts.get("rows"):
    rows = [calibre["rows"][int(i)] for i in opts["rows"].split(",")]
seed = None
if opts.get("seed") == "sea":
    seed = [{"lay": "sea", "row": int(opts.get("row", 2)), "quanta": int(opts.get("quanta", 20)), "sense": int(opts.get("sense", 1))}]
H.install(**sw)
orig = H.time_numerator
log = []
def traced(self, k):
    n = orig(self, k)
    if self.going == 1:
        at = (0, 0, 0)
        clk = self.clock
        g = self.trains[k][0]
        log.append((clk.ticks, int(H.x_of(clk, g.now)[at]), int(H.x_of(clk, g.before)[at]), int(self.v_kin[at]), int(carries(self.v_spr, clk.teeth)[at]),
                    int(carries(self.v_pot - self.v_spr, clk.teeth)[at]), int(self.rate[at]), int(carries(n, g.dteeth)[at])))
    return n
H.time_numerator = traced
c = H.clock_of(world, calibre_rows=rows, seed=seed)
for _ in range(ticks):
    c.tick()
print("tick X Xb K V G rate q")
for r in log:
    print(*r)
